4.1.1.2 Facilitate the moderation of the “three agro-issues” 

“Three agro-issues” refers to mainland China’s countryside issue, agriculture issue and farmer issue, as well as certain social issues derived from them, including the extreme disparity between the rich and the poor, floating population, etc. The farmer issue is the core of “three agro-issues”, which can be noticed as farmers have low income and difficulty in income growth, a considerable disparity between Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 86 the income of urban residents and the income of rural residents, and the interests of farmers cannot be sufficiently secured; the countryside issue can be mainly noticed as the underdeveloped features of countryside and underdeveloped economy, which can 

be summarized by a more figurative speech i.e. “cities look like Europe, countrysides look like Africa”; and the agriculture issue can be noticed as farmers cannot get appropriate profit from farming and industrialization level of agriculture is quite low. Chongqing can be described as a typical example of a major city that has a considerable rural area, which means “three agro-issues” is more noticeable in the case. The implementation of each sub-component under CURIP is capable of providing the townships and countryside where the projects are located with improved traffic, water supply, basic healthcare service and employment service conditions, which will definitely improve the living standard and health level of the people from the areas where each sub-component of CURIP trail projects is located and surrounding areas; rural reconstruction can improve the public service standard of countryside, considerably improve the features of the demonstration villages at trail spot, reduce the disparity between urban area and rural area, facilitate the moderation of “three agro-issues” locally, and hence discover certain experience in further planning the reform and development of urban and rural areas as well as setting an example. 

4.1.1.3 Remarkable social benefit and environmental benefit of CURIP 

a) Remarkable social benefit The implementation of each sub-component under CURIP established an sound foundation and an example for the economic growth in Chongqing’s rural area, reservoir area, mountainous area and minority area, which allows the places of such sub-components utilizing favorable infrastructure conditions such as road traffic and water supply so as to adjust industrial structure, develop dominant industries, facilitate the outgoing circulation of agricultural and sideline products, utilize the coverage of technology, economy, home appliances and chemical products formed by large and medium sized cities, promote the reciprocal development between urban and rural areas; the improvement of farmers’ employment skills can widen their income channels, provide more technical and quality talents to the city, reserve Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 87 

advanced techniques and developing concepts for returned migrant workers’ to establish their own business, and hence facilitate the growth of rural economy; the development of basic healthcare service and ecological environment can improve the health standard of farmers and create necessary conditions for developing countryside tourism, the road project can connect the abundant tourism resources, folk culture and religious culture along such roads together, while the maintenance and management after the construction and commission of the roads may also increase local employment opportunities; The increasing of passenger flow and freight flow will stimulate consumption and hence facilitate the development of third industry such as catering, service, folk artfact in countryside. Since the title of infrastructures such as the roads in those townships belongs to the townships themselves, it can be expected that relevant sub-components will have the volunteered support of such townships as well as farmers in land acquisition, relocation, waste disposal during construction stage as well as maintenance and management during operating stage. It is safe to say the implementation of each sub-component under CURIP focuses on providing infrastructure and service platform to villages, towns and countryside, which brings noticeable, practical and accessible benefits to farmers, hence it has remarkable social benefit. b) Remarkable environmental benefit Since each sub-component under CURIP is relatively small in scale and the majority of road and water supply projects is based on reconstruction and expansion, the extent and level of its adverse impacts such as land acquisition, relocation and ecological damage during construction stage are quite limited; water drawing, water supply, the construction of rural ecological environment and the construction of basic healthcare service are all in conformity to the requirements of clean production; both the construction and the operating process of each sub-component has feasible pollution control measures, protective measures for ecological environment are also applicable and feasible, all pollutants are discharged upon reaching specified standards; the projects only have slight impact on the atmosphere, surface water, acoustical Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 88 environment and ecological environment of relevant areas, and will not induce any significant changes on the environmental performance of project sites; the 

construction of the projects are in conformity to the state’s industrial policies as well as local government’s social and economic development plans; no significant environmental restrictions have been witnessed; the improvement on rural infrastructure such as road and water supply also improves the value-added utilization of rural land, while the protection of waterhead, the management and distribution of water resources are more scientific, also the conservation of soil and water indicates certain improvement; the completion and commission of the Mie’ao-Liangting Highway in Xiushan County, the Central Jinsha Road in Tongliang County as well as the urban sewage network and stormwater network along the roads make it possible to separate sewage from stormwater, and hence certain improvement on the water quality of the Meijiang River in Xiushan County and the Bachuan River in Tongliang County can be expected. The ecological environment projects of the demonstration villages of rural reconstruction corrected certain bad habits, which will serve as an effective demonstration and promotion of improving the ecological environment of countryside and reducing the diffused pollution in countryside. The construction of basic healthcare service system can provide farmers with healthcare service as well as carry out the deformation and collection of medical waste and then take the waste to detoxic treatment, and hence improve the pollution in soil and surface water caused by medical waste through surface runoff in countryside. The trial projects of water supply in townships also promoted the implementation of the proposed water pollution control in the said areas, for instance, the proposed sewage disposal plant in Huangshui County – stage 2 project (sewage disposal capacity is only 3,300m 3/day) may have an early completion ahead of its proposed date of completion and commission (2012). It can be concluded from the foregoing paragraphs that CURIP only has minor impacts on ecological environment during its construction stage and will not induce any significant changes in the environmental functions of project site; it also contributes to the value-added utilization of countryside land, the protection of Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 89 waterhead, and more scientific management and distribution of water resources as well as improved conservation of soil and water; the ecological environment of countryside can be improved, the diffused pollution in soil and surface water caused by the randomly discharged waste, sewage and medical waste through surface runoff can be moderated significantly; its implementation can also facilitate the implementation of environmental planning in villages, towns and countryside, all of which indicate that CURIP has remarkable environmental benefit. 

4.1.1.4 Exploring experience for the urban-rural integrated development of Chongqing and the entire state 

At late December 2008, the Prime Minister Wen Jiabao visited Chongqing and clearly 

indicated that Chongqing’s unique position must be recognized from an general perspective that covers the Development Drive of West China and the nation’s modernization drive; the executive meeting convened by the State Council on 31 December 2008 indicated again that accelerating Chongqing’s urban-rural integrated reform and development is significant to the further implementation of the Development Drive of West China, to the establishment an interactive development and reform pattern between coastal areas and inland areas, and to the protection of the ecological environment of the drainage area of the Yangtze River; The operational report released by the municipal government of Chongqing on 08 January 2009 suggests “implementing Certain Opinions on Facilitating the Urban-Rural Integrated Reform and Development of Chongqing with due diligence, and regarding the assurance of growth, the expansion of innate need, the adjustment of structure and the benefits to people’s livelihood as the theme of effort”, and further requires accelerating the integrated development of the One-Hour-Drive Economic Circle and the “two flanks” i.e. Northeast Chongqing and Southeast Chongqing, boosting the implementation of the traffic construction of the “two flanks”, integrating municipal financing to support the infrastructure construction in the small sized townships in the two flanks; it is also proposed to construct and renovate 8,000km highways in countryside, to reinforce the highways in townships and administrative villages respectively by 85% and 40%, increase methane users by 150,000 families, and complete 1,000 rural reconstruction plans. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 90 While offering more public services to the residents of townships and countryside, the implementation of CURIP trial projects also explored in various aspects to accumulate experience. The implementation, demonstration and promotion of CURIP can facilitate the interaction between urban resources and rural resources, which is beneficial to pilot the flow of production elements e.g. funds, techniques, talents and management from urban to countryside, participate in the reform experiments concerning the integration of the urban-rural complex, reduce the disparity between 

urban and countryside gradually, moderate Chongqing’s contradiction of a dualistic structure symbolized by a major cit and vast countryside, facilitate the economic growth and environmental improvement in Chongqing’s reservoir area, mountainous area and minority area, and hence accomplish the objectives of carrying out rural reconstruction as well as the construction of infrastructure such as road water supply in townships in the key areas of comprehensive renovation. Chongqing can be described as a place that has various features such as the reservoir region of Sanxia Dam, a typical countryside in West China. Road construction in townships under CURIP greatly improved the traffic in relevant districts and counties and made the production, living and trip of rural residents more convenient; training program for migrant workers is beneficial to the employment of surplus rural labor and public stability; water supply projects for townships, rural reconstruction and basic healthcare service are beneficial to the improvement of the ecological environment in countryside, the betterment of health standard in countryside, and the changes in rural environment; from the view of environmental safety it is beneficial to the continuous improvement of the aquatic environment of Sanxia Dam’s reservoir regions, and beneficial to the safety of the ecological environment of Yangtze River’s drainage areas. 

## 4.1.2 Water Environment Impact and Mitigation Measures 4.1.2.1 Water environment impact and mitigation measures during construction 

a) Impact on water environment Pollution of water environment during construction comes mainly from the domestic wastewater from construction camps and the production wastewater from the construction site, construction of bridges crossing a river, construction of tunnels, Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 91 construction of water intake and water producing structures, and laying of water supply pipelines. The major pollutants are COD, SS, and petroleum. The major pollutants in the production water are suspended substance and small amount of petroleum while those in the domestic wastewater are COD. Materials such as bitumen, oils, and powder materials, etc, which are piled in the construction camps or on construction sites near the water bodies, can be possibly flushed by a storm or strong wind into the water bodies due to improper keeping, causing pollution of the water bodies. The remnants in the spoiled construction dump yard and mixing yard may be carried over into the water bodies with ground stream runoff, resulting in pollution. There are about 10 households of residents at both the entrance and exit of Laodingyan Tunnel on the Huimin to Yipin Road, Banan District, and the domestic water for them is mainly supplied by a spring. The tunnel excavation and construction may result in certain impact on the underground water in the area of the tunnel within a short period, leading to falling of the underground water level in some sections, or even cutting off the existing water source from the spring supply, or difficulties in water supply because of pollution caused to the spring water due to the construction. The construction of a grand bridge across Meijiang River to be performed in Xiushan County Mie’ao to Liangting Road - Phase I may incur siltation in the downstream riverbed and deterioration of the water quality due to hole-drilling and pile-grouting processes, which may put out some drilling debris silting up the riverbed downstream and deteriorating the water quality, if they are left wantonly in the river. Therefore, apart from engineering precautions to be necessarily adopted to safeguard the flood passage in Meijiang River, the impact of SS and petroleum on the water quality, sightseeing and aqueous organism of the Meijiang River must be kept closely watched. Construction of buildings such as water supply intake pump houses may also have impact of SS and petroleum on the water sources; the soil from the excavation of pipe trenches during the construction of pipeline is likable to have impact on the surface water and landscape due to the flushing b rainwater or surface runoff. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 92 b) Mitigation measures Strengthen the management of material protection against rain and surface runoff scouring; put the bulk or loose materials in the enclosed barrack block or cover them properly; collect the construction debris or remnants in a timely manner; provide sedimentation basin at the construction site; provide catch basin at the tunnel construction site to settle the wastewater; maintain the structures and flush the vehicles; reuse possibly the wastewater resulted from equipment maintenance after sedimentation treatment; forbid direct discharge of boring debris and mud into the water bodies nearby; bury the boring debris locally above the highest water level line by means of a cofferdam; build emergency wastewater sedimentation basins at both ends of the grand bridges across Chongqing-Huaihua Railway and Meijiang River of Mie’ao to Liangting Road - Phase I in Xiushan County. The small quantity of wastewater from the construction sites shall be used for agricultural irrigation after being treated by some simple facilities such as a dry toilet. The oil used shall be reclaimed for selling; no waste shall be allowed to be discharged into or no solid substances be placed nearby the river or reservoir during construction in order to avoid the percolating water from polluting the ground water or surface water. As for protection of the environment of the water sources, we will adhere to the principle: Protection first, Prevention dominated, and Prevention and Treatment Integrated; during the construction of water supply intake utilities, harmful activities destroying water environment or endangering the protection of water sources shall be strictly inhibited, and construction wastewater shall not be allowed to be directly or indirectly discharged into the water sources. The construction camp shall be located far away from the water source protection area, so shall the temporary storage site of construction debris and solid waste; protect strictly the shelterbelt of forests or vegetation in the water source protection area; the construction contractor and supervisor shall strengthen the publicity of water source protection and strength the public supervision by establishment of information release system. Arrange reasonably the construction schedule: the earth and stone work construction of the road subgrade and that of the water supply pipeline shall be kept away from the Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 93 rain season. Damming and flood draining measures shall be adopted if the construction is going to be carried out in the rain season. According the experience in construction of tunnels in underground water development area, Laodingyan Tunnel on Huimin to Yipin Road, Banan District shall adopt pilot-hole water detection and water blocking measures during the constructional excavation of the tunnel in order to prevent natural ecosystem destruction such as underground water from rushing out in a large amount, dropping of the underground water level and collapsing of ground surface; to mitigate the impact of tunnel construction on spring water sources for the 10 households of peasants, the compensating source for the spring water at the exit of the tunnel shall be identified before the construction formally begins, proper sealing of the water burst and spring diversion shall be properly done in the process of construction so as to avoid interruption of the water sources, having impact on the water supply for the residents near the tunnel. 

4.1.2.2 Water environment impact and mitigation measures during operation 

a) Water environment impact 

There aren’t any service facilities in the road sub-component, therefore no wastewater will be produced during operation; the impacts on water environment during operation phase are the impact inflicted by the small amount of used oil or solid waste generated by the operation and maintenance of the raw water supply facilities in the water supply sub-component, the impact on the ground surface water inflicted by the small amount of discharged sludge water or backflush water for the filter pond in the water production process, and the impact of the domestic wastewater and medical wastewater from sub-components built in the community health care system as well as that of the wastewater produced during the practical training of the peasant workers. b) Mitigation measures (1) Mitigation measures for water supply sub-component Collecting barrels shall be used to store temporarily the small amount oil used or oil-containing solid waste from the water supply sub-component raw water-intaking Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 94 pump station for multipurpose usage; the small amount of sludge water from the water production process and the backwash water from the filter pond shall be collected and sent to gravity concentration tank for sedimentation and filtration, and, according the analysis of the supernate of sludge discharged from a similar water plant, the supernate after sedimentation has been removed of not only silt, but also COD; the COD concentration in the supernate is about 20mg/L, meeting the quality requirement of water source, and can be reused; therefore, the supernate will be sent 

back to the water plant’s mixing pond for reuse with reusing percentage of 95%, while some other small amount of wastewater to be discharged out of the battery limits contains mainly mud and sand upon drying treatment (see Fig. 4-1); the domestic wastewater from all of the water supply sub-components is of very small quantity and can be used for agriculture after dry toilet putrefaction (water supply for Huangshui Town), or be discharged after it is treated with anaerobic biochemical treatment (water supply for three towns in Xiushan County) to meet the standard (see Fig. 4-2); after the completion of the wastewater treatment plants as planned in Huangshui Town, Shizhu County, the domestic wastewater from the water supply sub-components shall be collectively treated in the wastewater treatment plants there. As Zhuyi Town Water Supply Project in Fengjie County will only transport and distribute water supplied from Wangjiaping Water Plant, there will be no industrial wastewater produced there.

> Domestic wastewate r

Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 95 (2) Mitigation measures for impact of medical wastewater from sub-component of community health care system The wastewater from sub-component of community health care system covers mainly the domestic wastewater and medical wastewater. The hospital wastewater is a kind of special wastewater, which is mainly polluted by the pathogens (e.g. ascarid eggs, hepatitis viruses, tubercule bacillus, diarrhea and other popular harmful bacteria) apart from some chemical pollutants. The wastewater comes mainly from the diagnosis and treatment offices, laboratories, wards, laundries, X-ray photograph rooms, and surgery rooms, containing large quantity of pathogenic bacteria, viruses, and chemical agents, characterized with space pollution and acute and latent infection. Currently, problems exiting in the treatment of wastewater from a hospital include complicated operational management, unstable system operation and difficulties in standard compliance etc. In the process of hospital wastewater treatment, it is highlighted to control strictly the pathogenic microorganism from being discharged with the wastewater. According to the related national standards and the economic and current technological conditions in our country, the principle for hospital wastewater treatment is to ensure the disinfection effect so as to meet the national standard for wastewater discharge. Currently, the treatment of wastewater from a hospital is mainly carried out by means of ozone electrolysis, chlorine disinfection, electrolysis of brine, and so on. However these technologies can only disinfect the hospital wastewater to meet the health standard of discharge, but can hardly meet the environmental discharge standard; such approach is featured by large investment, high cost, short service life and difficulties in treatment. Community Health Service Guidance and Administration Center in Yubei District has set up the two-stage wastewater treatment station, with medical wastewater being treated by SBR biological wastewater treatment processes + disinfection so as to meet the standard before discharge. Limited by the economic conditions, it is very difficult for a small-sized rural medical station to be established in the basic health care system to build a wastewater treatment station, so in the Assessment it is proposed that the small quantity of Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 96 medical wastewater and domestic waste may be treated in the purification biogas tank before it is discharged, thus possibly recovering the secondary energy and allowing the wastewater effectively treated. In recent years, with the development of anaerobic biochemical treatment technology, a feasible approach has been provided for the treatment of hospital wastewater. As for the treatment of wastewater from hospitals, the fact that the wastewater may carry the viruses and pathogenic bacteria of the infectious patents must be considered, so a common wastewater treatment measure such as a common wastewater purification 

tank can’t be used for such a treatment. However, anaerobic treatment integrated with fultative treatment shall be adopted as the major means instead, making use of a series of physical, chemical and biological principles to settle, decompose, swallow and kill the infectious bacteria and viruses. The water purified by means of a biogas tank can basically meet the health standard and environment standard. Table 4-2 has given out the inspection results of the hospital wastewater treated in an atmospheric biogas tank. It can be found from Table 4-2 that the effect of purification of a biogas tank for the hospital wastewater is apparent. The test results in Table 4-2 show: except that a few indicators fail to meet the standard, other major biochemical indicators of the outlet water from the purification biogas tank can meet the requirements in Water Pollutant Discharge Standard for Medical Institutions (GB18466-2005). Especially, the removal effect of manure coliform and parasite egg is remarkably good, better than the standard requirement. From above comparison analysis, it is a transitional selection with technical and economical feasibility for the town health care stations or village-level health care offices that have no conditions to establish a wastewater treatment plant to treat the wastewater with the purification biogas tank technology, with both environmental benefit and energy benefit. After adopting the above-mentioned measures, the impact of the basic health care system project on the water environment turns out be quite small, basically no impact on the function of the water bodies within the sub-component area. (3) The wastewater produced in the course of practical training of the migrant workers shall be treated in the existing water treatment facilities in each training base before it is discharged into the city drainage piping system so as to meet related standard. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 98

4.1.2.4 Mitigation measures for the induced wastewater impact from the water supply project during the operation period 

When the water supply project increases the water consumption at the project site, the water supply project is also inducing the increase or associated increase of the wastewater and sewage discharge volume at the project site, resulting in induced pollution impact on the water environment in the concerned areas. In order to mitigate the induced wastewater impact by the joint effect of the water supply sub-component, the countermeasures for environment treatment have been put forward from the aspect Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 101 of planning or environment assessment recommendations by the authorities of the districts or counties where the sub-components are located. ## 4.1.3 Environmental air quality impact and mitigation measures 4.1.3.1 Environmental Air Quality Impact Analysis during Construction Period and Mitigation Measures 

The pollutants for environmental air during construction period come mainly from powder mixing at the construction site, the uncompleted road surfaces, the storage yard, and the flying dust from the roads leading to the site, including the exhaust gas emitted from power machines and the bitumen smoke and fume from the construction of road pavement, where the dust and bitumen fume have most impact on the surrounding environment. The comparison analysis of pollution impact during the construction period shows that pollutant TSP in the construction period is distributed in such a general pattern as indicated in Table 4-5. Table 4-5 Variation of Pollutant TSP Concentration of during the Construction Period with the Change of Downwind Distances (mg/m 3 )Downwind distance from the construction site 50 100 150 Ordinary construction road surface or construction site 12.0 9.6 5.1 Dust or powder mixing (road mixing) 0.39 - 0.27 In order to mitigate the impact of construction dust and exhaust gas on the environment, the following measures shall be adopted to prevent air pollution during the construction period: (1) The mixing station shall be located at the downwind side of the predominant wind direction and the distance to the sensitive site shall be no less than 100m taking consideration of the economic transportation; (2) The transportation and storage of the powder materials shall be done with proper covering-up; (3) In the construction organization and design, duly sprinkling of water on the ongoing section of road, the temporary roads and the construction access roads must Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 104 be taken into consideration, allowing the road surface to be properly moisted so as to reduce the dust pollution (in dry weather in summer season or draught climate in autumn, water sprinkling can be done twice every day, one in the morning and one in the afternoon); bamboo fence or grass packing can also be employed in order to reduce the flying dust along the road stirred up by the running vehicles and wind; (4) The construction spoils of earth to be transported on the road shall not be allowed to contain too much water in order to avoid mud from leaking along the road and thus affecting the road cleanness and leading to secondary air-borne dust pollution; and (5) Strengthen the maintenance of the construction transportation machines so as to reduce the exhaust gas emission from the machines. 

4.1.3.2 Air environment impact and mitigation measures during operation period 

a) Impact of the road sub-components on environment air After completion of the road projects, the exhaust gas from cars is the major pollutant source for the environmental air pollution along the road. The emission rate increases with the traffic rate in proportion; furthermore, it is related to the car type and the pollution source intensity of the cars. It is predicted that after the reconstruction of Huimin to Yipin Road is completed, the quality of the environmental air along both sides of the road shall meet the Class 2 standard during the operation period, and the impact of the exhaust gas emitted from the cars at the environmentally sensitive sites along the road shall be very small within the areas in the assessment. According to the environment assessment for the other roads, in the early, middle and later period of operation, both the daily-averaged NO 2 concentration and the hourly peak concentration of NO 2 can meet the Class 2 standard of GB3095-1996 at the distance of 10m away from the road shoulders in every section along the road. b) Analysis of impact of water supply sub-component on environment air and the mitigation measures The impact of water supply plant on environmental air is mainly comprised of odor of the raw water, the smell of the remnant chlorine in the purified water disinfectation rooms, and the smell of the grate sediments. The water treatment facilities in the water purification plant are of open types, and the odor in the raw water will diffuse into the atmosphere, leading to impact on the surrounding environment on certain degree dependent dominantly on the factors such as the water volume and water quality. According to the statistics of investigation into smelling of the odors carried out by Shanghai Water Purification Plant, people can Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 106 smell the odor within a 20m distance downwind from the water treatment facilities, 

but can’t smell the odor beyond a 50m distance downwind the water treatment facilities. The treatment capacity of CURIP water supply sub-component is typically small, and the quality of the raw water is preferably good, so the impact of raw water odor is quite small according to the analogy analysis. To prevent the impact of grate sediments, the sediments shall be transferred to the city garbage treatment plant for disposal by means of tightly closed vehicles; the water supply plant in Huangshui Town has been provided with a air supply and exhaust ventilation system in the chlorination workshop in order to further reduce the impact of the remnant chlorine odor. The workshop air shall be led to an overhead position for venting by means of air duct; in addition, in order to prevent the odors from affecting the surrounding residents, the water plant is going to provide a 50m health protection distance. 

## 4.1.4 Acoustical environment impact and mitigation measures 

As no conspicuous noise is produced in the construction of subcomponents including the basic health care system, the reemployment training for the migrant workers and the new countryside construction, the major noise is mainly from the noise produced during the construction of road project and the running vehicles during operation period, from water supply sub-component construction and the production noise during the operation period. 

4.1.4.1 Acoustical environment impact and mitigation measures during construction 

Please see Table 4-8 for the noise levels of the road constructional machinery typically used in China when they are running at a full load at different distances. The environment assessment will be carried out for the noise impact of road and water supply sub-components during construction based on the practical conditions of the project. Table 4-8 Noise Levels of Major Construction Machinery at Different Distances Unit: Db (A)                                     

> Machinery Name 5m 10 m 20m 40m 60m 80m 100m 150m 200m Loader 90 84 78 72 68.5 66 64 60.5 58 Vibratory rollers 86 80 74 68 64.5 62 60 56.5 54 Bulldozer 86 80 74 68 64.5 62 60 56.5 54

Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 107 land grader 90 84 78 72 68.5 66 64 60.5 58 Excavator 84 78 72 66 62.5 60 58 54.5 52 Spreader 87 81 75 69 65.5 63 61 57.5 55 Mixer 87 81 75 69 65.5 63 61 57.5 55 Note: the noise levels at 5 meters are values measured actually. It can be seen from the above table and the interpolated calculation that single set of construction machinery without any shielding will have noise impact on the environment within a distance of 35m in day time and 200m at night, which can satisfy the Noise Limits for Construction Site (GB12523-90) beyond these distances. Except Tongliang County Road Infrastructure Project which covers more schools and hospitals (8 places) within a distance 35m from the centerline of the road, all the other roads cover few acoustically sensitive environment sites within 35m to the road sub-component and water supply project. During construction, except the loaders, ballgraders, the noise levels of which at a distance 20m away exceed the limits as specified in the Noise Limits for Construction Site when working in a manner of a single machine, the noise levels of other machines are lower than the noise limits as specified in Noise Limits for Construction Site .The following noise mitigation measures are going to be taken during the construction period: (1) Pay special attention to the maintenance of construction and transportation machinery in addition to reasonable operation, keeping the low noise level of the construction and transportation machinery; (2) Make reasonable arrangement so as to limit operation with several machines at the same time and restrict the high noise level machinery from being operated at night; (3) As to the construction of the road sections adjacent to the schools, hospitals and residence places, try not to use the high noise level machinery or equipments as possible. If such operation is inevitable, carry out the construction in the day when no teaching activities are going on in a school, or temporary baffle boards or dead walls shall be used to isolate the noise, which may be made up of spoiled soil compacted into a dam wall; Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 108 (4) Strengthen the construction noise management: in case the operation at night with noise have to be carried out near a residence area or the operation in the day with noise have to be carried out near a school such as concrete casting, the operation must be reported to the local environment and government authorities in advance and the public notice must be given upon approval. During the construction, the low-noise equipment shall be used and the operation during shall be shorten as possible, or measures like provisional baffle boards or dead walls shall be taken to mitigate the influence on the residents; (5) The transportation road for construction shall be selected as far as possible away from the school, hospital or residence area; and (6) Mammoth blasting shall not be carried out within the road section 100m away from ruin of Benzun Monastery on the No. 2 Road of sub-component of road infrastructure project in Banqiao Town of Yongchuan City. It shall avoid any vibration and noise impact on Elementary School of Benzun Village and Buddhism venue of Benzun Monastery ruin. As the road and water supply sub-components are of reconstruction and expansion nature and the construction scale is not big with small construction working field, therefore, the impact of road project along the line, water supply project construction boundary and the pipeline installation on the acoustically environmental sensitive sites can be effectively mitigated after the above-mentioned control measures are adopted. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 109

The noise in daytime within 30 meters on both sides of the Xiushan County Mie’ao to Liangting Road reaches the applicable 4a standard, but that at night fails to do so; the short-term impact of noise beyond 30m on both sides of the road reaches the applicable Class 2 standard about 60m away from the centerline, and the long-term impact of the same reaches the applicable Class 2 standard about 100m away from the centerline. As for the other road sub-components, the noise is lower than 70dB (A) in daytime, and lower than 55dB (A) at night within 30 meters away from the road curb, which satisfies the 4a standard for noise (Class 2 standard on noise shall also applies to the schools, hospitals, and other sensitive sites in acoustic environment within 30 meters away from the road curb both in daytime and at night); the short-term noise impact on all areas beyond 30 meters away from the road curb satisfies Class 2 standard of the 

Standard for Acoustic Environmental Quality both in daytime and at night. However, along with the increase of traffic, the long-term noise impact on the areas beyond 30 meters away from the road in Banan District may exceed the standard at night. The analysis and prediction on acoustical environment of each sub-component shows: all the roads will cause smaller noise impact on the surrounding environment during the operation, except for Class I road (4.072km) of the Xiushan County Mie’ao to Liangting Road - Phase I, the Class I secondary trunkline of Middle Jinsha Road in Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 113 Tongliang County (1.7km), and the Huimin-Nanpeng section (Class II road) of Banan District Huimin to Yipin Road, which are heavily loaded with traffic and therefore may cause louder noise due to the running vehicles during operation. As for Banan District Huimin to Yipin Road and Tongliang County Road Infrastructure Project, since sensitive sites in acoustic environment along the road sub-components are less than 30 meters away from the road and are not properly shielded with road cuttings and noise screens, the noise impact there exceeds the standard, which requires mitigation measures. (2) Noise mitigation measures for road sub-components during operation 

In consideration of the errors (±2.5dB) in noise prediction, no measure will be taken generally if the off-spec. value is no higher than 3dB in a sensitive site. According to analysis of the sound transmission process from the source to the receiving point, the traffic noise can be controlled via: controlling the noise radiation at the source and in the transmission process and protecting the receiving points. The principal measures are: 

  Optimizing the route selection, road slopes and road surface design during the engineering design; improving the construction quality of road surface during construction; strengthening maintenance during operation in order to keep the road surface integral, plane and smooth. 

  Providing greenbelts, reducer plates, speed restriction signs and horn-prohibition signs in the road sections where the sensitive sites are located; 

  Taking relevant management and treatment measures against the vehicles whose noise practically measured exceed the standard value; 

  Adjusting the function of the houses near the road curb; 

  Strengthening the management and maintenance of the road during the operation to avoid traffic jams caused by occupying the road against the regulations; 

  It is recommended that the road surface near a town or the environmental sensitive sites should be made into asphalt concrete surface; 

  Installing double-layered windows and sunlight screening windows in the student dormitory of the Media College adjacent to Middle Jinsha Road, in Baoxiang kindergarten adjacent to the road curb of Lvfeng Section of Tongyan Road, in the in-patient buildings of Pingtan Health Center (perpendicular to the road) in Pingtan Section of Tongyan Road, in Damiao Health Center in Hufeng to Damiao Section; Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 114 

  As for the scattered Residential Areas close to the road, to which 4a standard applies, practical monitoring shall be strengthened and mitigation measures mentioned above shall be adopted for the sensitive sites where the noise impact exceeds the value in the 4a standard; if the values measured in the scattered Residential Areas along Banan District Huimin to Yipin Road exceed the standard value, noise impact may be reduced in accordance with the recommendations given based on the EIA of the sub-components; 

  Establishing greenbelts on central partition belt and both sides of Xiushan County Mie’ao to Liangting Road - Phase I with densely-planted trees to reduce the impact of noise to the environment; meanwhile, monitoring shall be strengthening on households who are living above 2 nd floor and facing with road, locating within 30m from road curb of Mie ’ao and Yingjia Wuji; noise mitigation measures shall be taken in the sensitive sites where the impact exceeds the values in function area, such as installing double-layered windows or sunlight screening windows or sound shielding & ventilation windows; advising the residents to adjust the function of houses since the rooms near the road curb shall not be used as bedrooms. 

  Planning of control measures As for the residential land planned, the principle measures to prevent and control the noise impact include reasonable control of the distance between it and both side of the road, reasonable design of the building’s layout and noise reduction design for buildings. After the above-mentioned measures have been taken, the noise impact of road sub-components to each sensitive site and important point in the acoustic environment will be considerably relieved to satisfy the acoustical environment standard. b) Acoustic environment impact and mitigation measures during operation of the water supply sub-component Follow the predicting modes recommended in the Technical Guidance for Environmental Impact Evaluation -Acoustical Environment . Predict and calculate the impact of equipment noise to different locations with point sound source attenuation and sound level superposition mode. The maxi mum sound level of the equipment noise in the water supply sub-components shall be about 100dB (A), while the conservative sound insulating attenuation in the workshop or plant shall be about 20dB (A). Therefore, the quantity of work is small, and no large machines generating noise will be employed; the roads are planned to be Class IV highways in view of cost and practicability, with a designed driving speed of 10km/h; both the construction noise and the traffic noise during the operation can meet relevant requirements of the standard; furthermore, since the traffic there is unhindered and the pavement is hardened, the traffic noise impact, compared with that before, is reduced; The countryside drinking water projects in Qingshui Village of Gunan Town, Xishan Village of Sanjiang Town, and Chuijiao Village of Datong Town in Qijiang County shall make use of suction and gravity to divert the water, which shall be energy-efficient and substantially free of noise. New Countryside Construction in Wutu Village, Dazhou Town of Wanzhou District and New Countryside Demonstration in Qinglong Village, Xianyi, Chengkou County has little noise impact during construction and operation, which meet the Class 2 noise standard.

## 4.1.6 Impact on ecological environment and precautions and mitigation measures 

According to the characteristics of the CURIP sub-components, the possible impact on ecological environment shall be mainly developed during the construction of road and water supply sub-components and during the operation of the basic health care systems. 

4.1.6.1 Trend analysis of impact on ecological environment of relevant areas 

Because the CURIP sub-components only occupy a small portion of the land of the areas where they are located and most of the road sub-components are only to reconstruct and expand the existing road, the ground area and biomass there thus Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 122 changed is relatively small and the variation of ground runoff factor will not result in an remarkable impact on the surface water and ground water. Besides, the road sub-components shall be provided with flood-relieving drains and channels, which can also mitigate the soil loss in rain seasons in those areas. According to investigation, we know the CURIP sub-components are close to the towns or market centers, so wild animals are very few there due to the frequent visit of the human beings, and only polyphagous birds and small rodent animals can be found there. There is no rare animal. The project construction will, though the construction may scare some birds and animals, because no remarkable impact on the diversity of the organisms in those areas since the construction duration is short and construction area is small. 

4.1.6.2 Soil erosion and mitigation measures 

a) Analysis of soil erosion The CURIP sub-components that may induce the soil erosion are the road and water supply sub-components during the construction. The increased soil erosion mainly comes from the construction site of the mainbody works and areas including the temporary construction area and temporary waste storage area. Please see Table 4-13 for analysis on causes of soil erosion during the construction. Table 4-13: Analysis on causes of soil erosion during construction   

> Area Construction characteristics Erosion manner and extent
> Mainbody works Construction area 1) Excavators are used for excavation, producing temporary waste piles; 2) Hand-operated air drills, explosives and so on will be used for stonework excavation, producing large quantity of rock debris; 3) Loose slope is caused by excavating slope and filling up the low-lying land; 4) Loose stacking of soil and rocks along the pipe trenches before pipeline laying 1) Serious soil erosion may develop from the runoff scouring along the backfill soil brink and the temporary soil piles because of the loose structure; 2) The fresh face formed by the slope of excavation may develop medium water erosion or even serious gravity erosion in some particular slope, leading to a potential risk of landslide or collapse during construction and operation; The storage site of raw materials, processing area and construction It is used for processing and storing, and shelter are built for the buildings and materials during construction 2) Removal of the provisional buildings upon completion of construction, making the ground surface exposed. 1) Construction areas are concentrated, the construction machinery and building materials are stationed there, minor soil erosion is likely to occur; 2) Even though naked ground may be formed upon the completion of construction, the soil erosion is however very slight because the earth has already

Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 123 camp been compacted. Temporary waste storage area, borrowing area The ground surface is loose in structure due to large quantity of excavation and piling. The waste piled in ditches and valleys may primarily be hydro-eroded, so it can possibly develop mud-rock flow due to runoff of storm, resulting in serious soil erosion if no preventive measures for soil erosion are adopted. According to the calculation based on experiences and equations and the analogy analysis of and investigation on some of the similar projects in Chongqing City, the accelerated erosion modulus in the construction areas may be 2-5 times of the original geomorphic soil erosion modulus during the construction period. Taking Banan District Huimin-Yipin Road Project as an example, the soil erosion analysis is given in Table 4-14, and the soil erosion analysis for road and water supply sub-components during the construction period is given in Table 4-15. According to the analysis of the absolute value of soil erosion increased indicated in Table 4-15, the soil erosion mainly takes place in the construction period of the road sub-components. b) Impact of soil erosion to ecological environment The impacts of soil erosion on ecological environment mainly include the following items: (1) Vicious circle of soil erosion As the engineering construction may cut and destroy the natural landforms, damage the vegetation, increase the bare ground and impact the topsoil, which will create conditions for various erosions and thus develop vicious circle of soil erosion. (2) Impacting river’s flood discharging and deteriorating the environment for the hydro bios At the action of rainwater runoff, soil erosion will bring mud and sand into the river, and the silt in a river will increase, develop deposit in the riverbed and block the floodwater from being rapidly and smoothly discharged. This is unfavorable for the Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 125 downstream flood prevention and floodwater discharge and will worsen the environment for the hydrobios at the same time. (3) Influence on production and life Soil erosion will not only impact the forests and farming lands, but also affect the road traffic, river navigation, public facilities, and the residents ’ production and life, such as mud-rock flow and landslide, etc. (4) Impact on natural ecological scenery The exposed surfaces of cut and filling left by construction, together with the stone and soil piled everywhere, clash with surrounding natural ecological landscapes, which impacts the natural ecology. (5) Deteriorate the soil fertility and reduce the land resources Because of the destruction of the original ground surface and vegetation caused by the excavation and filling in the project construction, the soil eroded, due to the rainwater and its runoff, will take away nutritious elements in the topsoil, which will deteriorate the soil fertility and affect the growth of trees and crops, causing adverse impact on the regeneration and reutilization of the land resources. c) Mitigation measures on soil erosion The soil conservation measures for road and water supply sub-components shall be taken for those engineering sites likely to cause soil erosion, such as soil disposal site, borrow site, subgrade slopes and foundation, etc. The major measures are: (1) Optimize the design so as to reduce the permanently occupied land and temporarily occupied land; use the deserted land and slopes as much as possible; properly balance the excavation and filling of soil and rock and reasonably allocate them in the course of construction management; provide additional retaining wall or foot guard for the road section with fill height higher than 5 meters. (2) In the process of leveling of the bridge foundations, tunnels and grounds, it is forbidden to discharge waste into the surface water. Before the waste disposal site is used, waste retaining dam shall be built in the downstream in order to prevent the abandoned soil or waste from running into the river course or burying the roads and farmland; water catchment ditches shall be provided at the upstream, with drains provided on both sides of the waste disposal site. The catchment ditches will direct the water from the upstream into the drains on both sides of the waste disposal site, and then discharge it into the downstream. This will avoid direct scouring and erosion of the abandoned soil and waste by the water from the upstream. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 126 (3) The pipe trenches shall be cut and backfilled at different layers; the topsoil, except for the storage site with dry-stone water catchment drains and a sedimentation tank around, shall be temporarily covered with color-strap cloth. (4) Drains shall be provided outside the borrow site in order to prevent the water from the top of the hill from scouring and eroding the borrow slopes. (5) Upon completion of the works, the temporarily occupied land for construction and waste disposal shall be reinstated, so shall the exposed sections resulted from excavation and the borrow site; trees and grass shall be planted on the acquired land, and retaining dams or flood prevention and draining facilities shall be properly provided. As the environmental protection measures, such as side ditches, reinforced slopes, retaining walls, road solidification, site and revetment greening, prevention and protection for spoil yards and vegetation reinstatement, have been fully taken, the soil erosion at the construction site will be effectively controlled and the regional ecological environment will be substantially restored as soon as possible after the completion of construction. 

4.1.6.3 Analysis of impact on land utilization 

According the stipulations with regard to the administration of land utilization, the land utilization can be divided into 8 types, arable land, garden lot, forestation land, grassland, residence area, mining land, traffic land, water field, and unused land. The current utilization of land along the lines or at the sites selected for the CURIP are mainly traffic land, arable land, forest land, residence area and water field. Along the line where road sub-components are planned to be built, there is mostly agricultural ecology with a small content of city ecology. Besides the traffic land and barren hill slopes, the agricultural land covers mainly the arable land and cash-crop land; on the arable land, there are mainly corn, rice, wheat, and other grains, while on the cash-crop land, there are vegetables with small quantity of fruit trees; there is rarely concentrated arable land, and along the mountains or the pipeline of water supply sub-components, most of the arable land is scattered among the mountains or distributed in straps along gullies. 4.1.6.4 Summary of the impact on ecological environment 

Since the area of land occupied by the CURIP sub-components takes a small proportion in the regional environment, there will be no remarkable changes in the agricultural ecosystems on the periphery of sub-components; except that some of the landforms, terrains, and ground surfaces may be changed, the regional biological Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 129 diversity, the wood reserve, the surface water and the ground water shall remain unchanged. The provision of flood drains and channels for the road and water supply sub-components and the vegetation restoration after the construction can ease to certain degree the soil erosion in the affected areas in rain seasons, and promote the water conservation level; in addition to the recovery of the vegetation destroyed in the construction period, the water supply sub-components have also strengthened the protection of ecological environment in the water source areas; the sub-components of basic health care system and demonstrative villages of new countryside construction can gradually improve the disposal of countryside medical waste and domestic wastewater, and the current soil pollution and watercourse situation. To sum up, the CURIP sub-components will only impact the surrounding ecological environment during the construction period to a very small degree, and may, to some extent, improve the local ecological environment in the project-covered areas upon the completion of the project. 

## 4.1.7 Analysis of impact on social environment and the mitigation measures 4.1.7.1 Impact of land occupation and the mitigation measures 

The CURIP’s city road sub-components are located in hilly areas, and except Xiushan road sub-component, the road sub-components in Banan District, Tongliang County and Yongchuan District are mainly to reconstruct and expand the existing roads, so the arable land occupied for the roads accounts for a small proportion of the total arable land in those areas. Reserved road for construction has been planned for most of the existing 13 roads for Road Infrastructure Project in Banqiao Town of Yongchuan City, and only a few sections of road there may demand additional land. The land along the roads belongs to the whole village, and the land to be occupied will involve 1038 households in 90 villager teams of 12 villages along the lines; the land can be coordinated within the villages and communities; by June 1, 2009, all of the land readjustment has been finished, and the average arable land per capita has been changed from 1.084 mu before the readjustment to 1.062 mu after the readjustment. The arable land along No. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 130 6 Line where the impact is the largest, and the average arable land per capita here has been changed from 0.701 mu to 0.669 mu. The structures and buildings for the water transmission and distribution pipeline and pump works in Zhuyi Town, Fengjie County occupy about 1.236ha of land, accounting for approximately 0.0015% of the total arable land in Fengjie County. The permanently occupied land of Huangshui Water Supply Plant in Shizhu County is about 7.5 mu, where 5.5 mu was acquired in 2000 while the remaining 2 mu is woodland of weed trees. The roads construction for new countryside construction are mostly cutoff or straightening-up of the existing roads, so the land occupied will be to certain degree smaller; the expansion of the drinking water works, village service centers and the garbage collection will be basically made on the existing land. According to the site investigation by the EIA agencies and the investigations by the cultural heritage departments and armed-forces departments in various districts and counties, all of the sub-component construction sites (including those along the construction line) will not involve any cultural heritage protection site and military administration areas; no important optical cable, electrical cable, power transportation line, gas pipelines, or water mains will be demolished or relocated. There are only ordinary LV electrical lines and communication lines. The impact of construction on agricultural ecosystems shall mainly come from the permanent and temporary occupation of land. Because the sub-components have already evaded the center areas of the town or 

village during site and route selection, it will occupy farming land and farmers’ houses as few as possible and reduce the demolition and relocations, trying not to disturb the adjacent villages and towns. Upon completion of the sub-components, the temporary occupied land for access road, material storage, and pipeline laying can be reused, so can the soil disposal site and the borrow site after being covered by soil, hence reducing the impact of land occupation. As for the permanently acquired area and its peripheral areas, the production and operation mode shall be transferred from agricultural mode to urban-rural integration mode; land occupation will inevitably cause impact on the original traditional agriculture in addition to the reemployment of the farmers whose land has been acquired. The CURIP will speed up the adjustment Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 131 of agricultural structure and the industrialization of the agriculture, promote the development of modernized agriculture such as sight-seeing agriculture, ecological agriculture, and high-efficiency agriculture. With the help from the government, the rural surplus labor resulted from the land acquisition for such sub-components shall receive reeducation in the process of agricultural industrialization and urban-rural integration development so as to improve their reemployment ability under the new situations in order to get new development and job opportunities in the emerging secondary and tertiary industries and urban-rural integration development, enhancing the development of rural areas and the living standards of farmers. After completion of the construction of the roads, the land along the roads will gain a value-added utilization, which can promote the local land development, speed up the introduction of advanced agricultural technology, further improve the ecological environment of farmland, optimize the plantation structure, increase the unit yield of crops and the income of the farmers, and realize the transformation of land resource values. 

4.1.7.2 Construction impact and mitigation measures 

(1) Impact of access road The access road will temporarily occupy the land, which may deteriorate the acoustic environment and the air for the residents along the lines, destroy the vegetation, which is likely to cause soil erosion and impact the ecosystems. Mitigation measures include trying the best to provide access road within the acquired land, trying to evade the Residential Areas, schools, hospitals, and the sections with beautiful natural environment and good natural vegetation during the site selection, designing and construction. (2) Impact of using the existing roads Occupation of the existing roads by the construction vehicles will increase traffic load, affect the travel of the residents and communication of vehicles along these lines; the dust and the noises caused by the construction vehicles will impact the working and studying environment of the residents, schools, and hospitals nearby, laying a potential safety risk. Mitigation measures include carefully and reasonably selecting the transportation lines upon utilizing an existing road in order to avoid passing through the densely populated areas and villages; coordinating with the local government so as to avoid Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 132 traffic jams on the existing road; reasonably scheduling the construction to reduce the impact on the life of villagers along the line; providing remarkable warn signs or 

pedestrian’s passages where the construction vehicles appear frequently. (3) Impact of blasting Blasting may endanger the safety of the villagers, buildings and structures, and blasting for a tunnel may impact the underground water. Mitigation measures include providing the construction personnel with helmets and other necessary personal protection equipment; investigating the hidden safety risks within an area 500 meters from the blasting point; using and safekeeping the explosives in accordance with the safety requirements of the public security offices; thoroughly checking relevant matters before the blasting; duly evacuating the persons and vehicles out of the risk areas; making sure the construction activities will not damage the public service facilities. As for the reconstruction and expansion of No. 2 Line in Road Infrastructure Project in Banqiao Town of Yongchuan City, mammoth blasting or construction with large construction machinery shall be forbidden within road sections 100m away from Elementary School of Benzun Village. (4) Impact of construction camp All of the factors, such as the drinking water source, wastewater draining, domestic waste storage and disposal, and the civilization degree of the construction workers at the construction camp, may impact the environment. Mitigation measures include providing dry toilets or septic tanks at the construction camp, and using the putrefied sewage as organic fertilizer; discharging no wastewater from the construction camp directly into the natural receptacle waterbodies; collecting the solid waste on the construction camp regularly, and sending it to the designated municipal solid waste disposal site or station for treatment or disposal; making sure the drinking water on the construction camp has reached the national quality standard for drinking water; controlling the diseases of the construction workers, especially by means of education on prevention and control of some epidemic diseases. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 133 4.1.7.3 Analysis on impact of induced traffic and mitigation measures a) Analysis on impact of induced traffic The impact of induced traffic mainly comes from the road sub-components to be constructed, which is divided into favorable impact and adverse impact. Adverse impact mainly falls in the construction period, while favorable impact mainly falls in the operation period. The adverse impact in the construction period mainly covers: (1) It will increase the traffic load of related roads. 

  The vehicles that originally ran on the road to be reconstructed will have to change its route and run on another road not to be reconstructed, therefore, such unreconstructed road will be overburdened by the increased traffic; 

  Transportation of the construction materials, construction machiery, equipments, earth and rock will increase the traffic load of related roads; (2) Impact on the surfaces of related roads The overloaded transportation of some heavy-duty machinery and soil and rocks during the construction period may affect the road surface quality and shorten the maintenance and service terms of the roads; (3) Impact on the air environment and acoustic environment of related road sections. Due to the transportation of engineering equipment and materials, it may arouse additional dust and traffic noise, causing additional impact on the air environment and acoustic environment along related roads; (4) Additional impact on the social environment of related road sections Due to the increased traffic rate, dust, mud and noise in related road sections, traffic jams may be increased, which will cause inconvenience to the production and life of the residents there. b) Mitigation measures for induced traffic rate impact To mitigate the impact caused by the induced traffic during the construction period, the road construction contractor should make careful and overall site investigation on the induced road traffic during preparing the construction management plan in order to work out a scientific and reasonable construction transportation management plan based on the transportation capacity of related roads. For example, the transportation of bulk materials and equipment shall try to avoid passing the trade centers or trading days; eye-striking bypass route signboards should be positioned at the entrance and Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 134 exit of a road under reconstruction; overspeed and overload running of the construction vehicles shall be prohibited; the transportation route shall be reasonably selected to be away from the schools, hospitals, centralized residence centers and other acoustically sensitive sites; washing facilities for transportation vehicles shall be provided at the exit of construction site; the bulk materials shall be transport with proper cover. By taking the above-mentioned measures, the adverse impact of induced traffic rate during the construction period can be possibly mitigated to certain degree.
