Total 101 418 16023.6 The goal of the Emigrant Resettlement Policy of the World Band is to ensure that all the emigrants incurred by the projects can be benefited from the projects, that their living standard may be enhanced or at least recovered, and that their ability to gain income and their production may be assisted, especially for the particularly poor people. The resettlement policy and regulation on project emigrants in our country is Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 136 

consistent with the goal of the World Bank’s emigrant resettlement policy. b) Compensation policies, the goals and principles of emigrant resettlement The project emigrant resettlement shall be arranged by the sub-component PIUs according to relevant land and house relocation compensation policies of the State, compensating the residents evacuated by certain amount of land acquisition fee and resettlement compensation fee. According to related stipulations under the Law of Land Administration of the People’s Republic of China , the Land Acquisition Compensational Resettlement Measures of Chongqing , the Circular of Chongqing 

Municipal People’s Government on Adjustment of Land Acquisition Compensation and Resettlement Policy and the implementation rules on the compensation for acquired land and resettlement in all districts and counties, full-amount compensation shall be made for acquiring collective-owned land in the countryside in accordance with the laws and regulations. The major principles of resettlement and compensation are: (1) Ensuring that the people affected by the project can get full amount of compensation for loss before the their resettlement; giving them financial subsidy and help to overcome the transient difficulties in resettlement and production restoration; (2) Ensuring that all the people affected by the project can recover and reach the living standards, production capabilities and income levels before their resettlement when the resettlement is completed. (3) Compensating all of the people and buildings negatively affected by the projects within the project areas; as for some particular persons whose residence registration procedures, house-renting procedures, operation licenses, or the legal documents for buildings are not complete, no refusal of compensation or help shall be made for them on such account. (4) Giving reasonable compensation for the land acquired or occupied or the loss thus caused. All payment for land acquisition shall be paid in full amount within three months after the land acquisition and resettlement proposals have been formally approved, but no later than the date when such land is actually been employed for the project; all labor affected in the land acquired must be reasonably resettled so as to prevent the living standards from dropping down due to the land acquisition and resettlement. (5) Paying attention to the complaints from the people affected, and duly and Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 137 legitimately help them solve the difficulties and inconveniences arising in the course of the land acquisition and demolition & resettlement; any dispute of the emigrants against the compensation standards shall be resolved through friendly consultation or negotiation as possible, and it may also be solved via arbitration or litigation if such consultation or negotiation fails. (6) The residents to be resettled shall be compensated in terms of lump-sum payment in cash; (7) From the date of announcement of the Lan d Acquisition Circular, the male at 50 years old or above and the female at 40 years old or above, who are considered the aged labors, may apply for personal-savings type of social pensions; subject to the approval and review by the local state-land bureaus and housing administration agencies, a small amount pf money (e.g. RMB 19,200 in Tongliang County) out of the compensation for land acquired and resettlement shall be handed over to the insurance company for personal savings type of social pension, while the remaining shall be given directly to such affected persons; as the male above 16 but below 50 years old and the female above 16 but below 40 years old living in urban areas, who are formerly rural residents, are classified into labor force (reemployment), the compensational shall be all given to themselves, and all such persons can receive the vocational technical training for reemployment organized by the labor security agencies and enjoy relevant preferential reemployment policies for the laid-off and unemployed people. c) Impact on the life quality of the people resettled At the beginning of demolition and resettlement, their living standard may be affected due to the housing, transportation and job; because this road sub-components are the improvement of the infrastructure in small towns, which do not require high technology, it will provide a lot of labor-intensive jobs, and meanwhile, due to the sub-component construction, related industries such as transportation, auxiliary processing and service industries will develop accordingly, bringing abundant job opportunities for the emigrants; in fact, as for the urban residents or peasants located in the border between the urban and rural areas, they have wished to cast off the ill operation environment and the restriction by the natural economy of the countryside in the process of urbanization before; the CURIP, especially the road sub-components, is objectively meeting the demands of such people: they get the starting fund in terms of compensation for demolition and resettlement. The ratio of support Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 138 from the people from the affected areas shows: the resettlers understand and support the sub-components, and they can quickly overcome the short-term drop of life quality. Their life quality and educational level can be remarkably improved and enhanced via the reemployment after demolition and resettlement. Take Banan District Huimin to Yipin Road Reconstruction for example. All of the 11 houses demolished are old and dilapidated one-floor houses without toilets and heating systems and they provide a poor environment for living; after demolition and resettlement, the affected households can get the cash compensation due to the project, and farmland will be readjusted within their villages, so their production level, income level and living standards can be recovered to or surpass the previous ones with the help of such money. The cash compensation for resettlement may help improve their economic conditions and enhance their life quality. d) Potential impact of permanently acquired land and the mitigation measures The life and employment of the peasants without land after the land acquisition shall be the major problem caused by permanently acquisition of land. The peasants loosing the land may choose to be compensated in terms of cash or reallocation of land; the villages where reallocation of internal land is practical will normally reallocate the land, while those where reallocation of internal land is impossible can get land reallocated in neighboring villages, provided that the distance shall be properly controlled, i.e. no more than 2000m for Tongliang County. Because the roads and water supply pipelines have properly detoured the villager residence centers, with less occupation of land and less demolition & resettlement; the reusability of the temporarily occupied land must be taken into consideration at the design stage (the access roads can be used as tractor roads or walkways; reinstate the farmland in each site where pipelines have been laid; restore the vegetation after topsoiling the borrow site and soil disposal site), in order to reduce the land acquisition as much as possible; within the areas where the land has been permanently acquired and surrounding areas, the production and operation mode focusing on agriculture will be gradually transformed into urbanization, which will inevitably impact the original traditional agriculture in addition to the life and employment of the peasants without land. Construction of the road and water supply sub-components Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 139 will accelerate the adjustment of agricultural structure and the industrialization in surrounding areas, promote the development of modernized agriculture such as sight-seeing agriculture, ecological agriculture and high-efficiency agriculture. As for the rural surplus labor due to land acquisition, the competent agencies in the districts and counties will provide them with the environment to upgrade reemployment capacity and guide and organize them to receive re-education in the course of agricultural industrialization and urbanization of the countryside so as to upgrade reemployment capacity in the new situation and obtain new development opportunities and job opportunities in the emerging secondary and tertiary industries. e) Impact on infrastructure and mitigation measures The demolition of the existing low-voltage electric poles and ordinary telecom poles in the countryside due to the sub-component construction may impact to certain degree the power supply along the lines in a short time; the contractor shall carefully arrange the demolition of the poles, wires and cables and the update the construction schedules, taking into consideration of the materials, transportation, loading and unloading, safeguard and even the weather; give notice prior to the demolition and give notification to each family, try to recover the power supply as soon as possible so as to reduce the impact of the construction of electrical infrastructures to a minimal extent. Before the new water supply sub-component is put into service, even a short interruption of the existing water supply facilities shall bring a lot of inconvenience for the life and work of the residents within the service areas. The design and construction will optimize the replacement of existing water supply facilities with newly built ones, so as to shorten the replacement duration and reduce the areas affected by such replacement. f) Impact on vegetation The destruction of the vegetation resulted from demolition and resettlement is mainly the occupation of arable land, gardens and woodland, making vegetation even less. Most of the vegetation affected is of agricultural nature, its recovery ability is very strong and the recovery term is relatively short, so the vegetation on the temporarily occupied land can be rapidly recovered after the completion of the construction; the plants and the flowers on the slope, on the road and along the lines will improve the Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 140 ecological diversity along the areas to certain degree, while the coverage of vegetation is expanded; in addition, thanks to the road and water supply sub-components, some barren land will be developed and utilized with value added, so the soil erosion will be effectively controlled. In a word, the adverse impact on vegetation caused by the sub-components to be constructed will be very small after they are put into operation. g) Impact on disadvantaged group and mitigation measures In CURIP, there are 10 households of 33 disadvantaged persons who are disabled or weak will be involved in demolition and resettlement. The land acquisition for Longchi Town Water Supply Project in Xiushan County will impact one particularly poor family of 1 persons; the land acquisition for the Xiushan County Mie’ao to Liangting Road - Phase I will involve 6 disabled and low-income families of 24 

persons in Mie’ao, Guanzhuang Town., 1 household of 3 persons in Lushan villiage and 2 household of 5 persons in Zhengjia Villiage for Yubei Community Healthcare Services System. As for the disadvantaged group, in addition to the compensation as per the policies, following preferential assistance measures will also be taken: select the areas where their houses are located and conduct occupational trainings preferentially, provide job opportunities and reimbursable service preferentially; provide minimum living allowance subject to personal application, village’s and town’s certificate. As for the 1 household in Lushan Village, and the 2 households in Zhengjia Village, Yubei District, the poor families due to health problems, the PIUs - the People’s Hospital of Yubei District, the Land Reserve Center of Yubei District, Airport New-City Administration Committee, Shuanlonghu Street and relative village committees -will help them to rebuild their homes as quickly as possible by means of compensation, resettlement and land contraction to make their living standards much better than that before the demolition and resettlement; as to the 1 extremely poor family in Shuaijia Village, Longchi Town, Xiushan County and 6 disabled low-income families in Mie’ao, Guanzhuang Town, the recovery assistance to them will be intensified to enable them to restore and improve their production and living standards as quickly as possible with the compensation to them. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 141 

## 4.1.8 Feasibility Analysis to the Mitigation Measures 

With application of the above integrated preventive and mitigation measures, timeline of each Sub-component and discharge of waste water during operation planned in the 

CURIP can meet the requirements of the “Integrated Wastewater discharge Standard” (GB8978-1996); waste water discharge during operation of the public health service system Sub-component can meet the requirements of the “Discharge standard of water pollutants for medical organization” (GB 18466-2005); water supply Sub-component brings forward the short term or long term activities or proposals to the influences of reducing and inducing waste water; demonstration village of new countryside can treat the recycling biogas with waste water, which adjusts the countryside fuel structure and meanwhile is good to the vegetation cover in the countryside to improve the view of rural area. With the simple and applicable preventive measures of sprinkling, shielding and setting windward mixing point at fixed distance, the construction will have no obvious influence to the air quality in Sub-component area; as far as the road project is concerned, average daily concentration and hourly peak concentration of the main air pollutant NO 2, both can meet the second level of GB3095-1996 10m beyond the roadside; Since most of the road and water supply Sub-components are upgrading and extension plans, the project is in fact not so large and construction area is comparatively small. With integrated controlling measures, such as setting up enclosure, the influences to the environment sensitive points along the road and water supply projects construction area boundary as well as the pipeline laying areas are reduced; With measures of speed limiting, no horn signing and sound insulating etc., noises to the sound sensitive points along the line of Sub-component of the Huiyi road of Banan District and road infrastructure construction of Tongliang County Road Infrastructure Project as well as other concerned points can be largely reduced and meet the requirements of noise standards; With the measures of setting the noise sources like water pumps inside plant halls, installing sound muffling and vibration reducing facilities and setting green belts in open areas, the noise inside the plant area of water supply Sub-components can meet the second level of the limit of “standard of noise at boundary of industrial enterprises” (GBl2348-2008). After resource utilization and treatment procedures, influence of the solid wastes to environment is well controlled; with protective measures of side ditch, side slope and retaining walls etc., as well as environmental protection measures of road hardening, area greening, Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 142 slope greening, spoil field construction protection and vegetation recovering etc., the soil erosion state in construction area will be well controlled and the regional ecological environment will be recovered as soon as possible after the construction finishes; in areas of the road, public health service and new countryside demonstration village areas, the regional environment will be improved to certain degree due to better soil and water conservation, orderly treatment to medicare wastes as well as treatment or processing to garbage and domestic waste water. Chongqing has adopted a method to deal with medical waste in seperate areas. In this way, medical waste in the nine major districts are collected and treated by Chongqing Tongxing Medical Waste Treatment Company. Located in the Wuxing Village, Tongjiaxi Town, Beibei District, Chongqing Tongxing Medical Waste Treatment Company was founded in July, 2004 with a total investment reaching 30million. It has two medical treatment lines with daily capacity of 20t, which is consisted of transportation, incineration, gas treatment, on-line survey, and ash collection systems. The company makes small profits through its stable operation. Company status and its bussiness license see to annex B. Medical waste of Yubei community health care system under CURIP will be treated by Chongqing Tongxing Medical Waste Treatment Company. In addition, the community healthcare services system subcomponent, which includes 687 rural clinics (equipment procurement) and 37 community healthcare stations (equipment procurement) will have the medical / hazardous waste be treated by units established in Fuling District, Wanzhou District, Changshou District and Bishan County in harmless process so as to effectively control the adverse environmental impact. It ’s recommended to build the wastewater treatment station of community health care guidance center in the south within red line area(see annex B); the elevation should be lower or close to height of bottom of Dashiba Reservoir which will be easy for treated wastewater going to urban sewage system and preventing the IV class basin from polluting. It’s recommended to transfer medical and domestic wastewater into purifying biogas pool for treatment and up-to-standard discharge in close proximity if the sewage is in Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 143 small volume and easy to be treated. Those measures are feasible in technology and economy and applicable for small-scale rural healthcare stations, clinics and 10 community healthcare stations under Yubei subcomponent. In a word, mitigation measures aiming at reducing the bad influences to the environments during construction and operation periods are simple, proper and applicable, which are easy for implementation and supervising management.

Low investment Recommendation Note:: chlorine dioxide (ClO 2 ) is a kind of strong oxidant. It produces during disinfection no organic by-products. It kills germs and meanwhile removes Fe 2+ ,Mn 2+ and bad smells, which is more and more widely used for city water disinfection in recent years; The industries in Xiushan area is mainly electric-mineral industries producing Electrolytic Manganese, hence the Fe 2+ , Mn 2+ volume is relatively higher in ground surface water. Applying chlorine dioxide for disinfection not only considers about its disinfection function but also more effects according to the water property in Xiushan County; Besides, shortages of the local produced chlorine dioxide equipments in the past (such as instable capacity, small capacity of single equipment, low degree of automation) have been overcome with the development of science and technology. The chlorine dioxide disinfection used in Xujiaao water treatment plant of Xiushan County (30000m 3 /d) works well. ## 4.3 Potential Risks and Preventive Measures 4.3.1 Potential risks to water environment and preventive measures 

The risks to water environment during running of CURIP project mainly exist in pollution to ground surface water, earth and vegetation that may come from the unqualified transportation of dangerous chemicals in road project. Taking the sensitive road section of the bridge and tunnel in Huiyi Road, Banan District as an example, we analysed the probability of pollution to the ground surface water and surrounding environment caused by traffic accident of trucks with Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 150 poisonous chemicals during its passing through the environment sensitive road sections. The length of ground surface water sensitive road relating Huiyi Road and Bridge project is totally 516m and that relating the tunnel is 600m. When traffic accidents happen at the bridge and tunnel on trucks transporting dangerous chemicals during operation, safe egress is difficult, which may arouse damage to human lives; meanwhile, the ground surface water, soil and vegetation of the ecological environment near the bridge and tunnel will be damaged, air be polluted and even the bridge and tunnel be damaged, hence the whole road will be stopped. Probability of pollution by traffic accidents on trucks transporting dangerous chemicals at sensitive road in prediction years refer to table 4-19. Table 4-20 Probability of pollution by traffic accidents on trucks transporting dangerous chemicals at sensitive road of Huiyi Road, Banan District Unit: Times/Year            

> Prediction item Sensitive road Year of 2010 Year of 2016 Year of 2024
> Bridge (516m) 0.018 0.027 0.024 Accident probability %Tunnel of Laodingyan (600m) 0.027 0.042 0.038

Seeing from the above table, probability of traffic accident on trucks transporting dangerous chemicals at the sensitive roads of bridge and tunnel of Huiyi road, Banan District is relatively small, which decreases with the increasing of traffic and management and equipment level. By the year of 2024, the average yearly probability of pollution accident is less than 0.04 times/year. Although the probability of pollution accidents by transporting dangerous chemicals on sensitive road sections in the road project is relatively small, the result is severe, for which the preventive measures should not be looked over. The environment assessment has designed the preventive and mitigation measures for risks of polluting ground surface water by transporting dangerous chemicals and set up emergency pre-plans. The main preventive measures including setting up buffering belt to mitigate ground surface water pollution at sensitive road sections, such as bridge etc., setting up enhanced safety barrier and accident waste water settlement pool at both ends of huge big bridges etc. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 151 

## 4.3.2 Potential risks of disinfection agent, liquid chlorine, leakage of water supply sub-component and preventive measures 

To secure water supply quality in the water supply sub-components under CURIP, the water supplied from the treatment plant should be disinfected. The disinfecting agent used in the Huangshui Town Water Supply Project in Shizhu County is the liquid chlorine. A chlorine feeding room is designed in the project equipped with chlorine recycling machine as well as its matching facilities. The chlorine feeding volume is about 2.5mg/L. According to “Identification of major hazard installations” (GB 18218-2000) and “Technical guidelines for environmental impact assessment” (HJ/T 169 -- 2004), chlorine belongs to class I poisonous material, and limit volume in production site is 10t and in storage area is 25t. The water supply sub-component in Huang Shui Town is 0.6t/d, and the maximum chlorine storage capacity in the chlorine feeding room is 5.48t (for a whole year), hence the chlorine feeding room is not a great danger source and locates in non-sensitive area. Hence risk recognition and influence analysis are made according to level II evaluation and preventive, mitigation and emergency measures are put forward. Main measures are as below: 

  Strictly separate the production dangerous zones; make the layout according to 

production characters with safety and health are secured; consider about wind factors and safe protection distance. According to explosure and fire hazard level of the chlorine feeding room, select corresponding explosure preventive electric sub-components and equipments. 

  Design the buildings and structures according to fire protection level and 

production characters. Chlorine feeding room and liquid chlorine steel bottle use closed building with mechanical air intake facilities and chlorine gas treatment facilities to enhance ventilation and extracting poison to prevent concentration of chlorine gas in lower part of the plant. 

  Design of the chlorine feeding room should cons ider about safe egress and escape on accidents. 

  Design, manufacturing, testing and constructing and installation of the chlorine 

feeding room should strictly accord to relating standards with safety valves and explosure protection facilities being installed. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 152 

  According to property of chlorine gas, all relating equipments, pipelines, steel 

bottles etc., should be equipped accordingly with safety facilities of fire protection, explosure protection, poison protection, supervising and alarming equipments etc. 

  Liquid chlorine steel bottle storage warehouse should set a closed operation room 

with accident treatment system and chlorine recycling facilities for accident treatment, which uses radial-flow combined tray and multiple stage liquid storage technology with maximum absorbing capacity of 500kg/h. 

  During the design of chlorine feeding room, the intensity of the earthquake should 

be considered as VII degree and safety measures should be fully considered. 

  Automatic chlorine supervising and alarming syst em should be installed in the chlorine feeding room. When the chlorine density inside the feeding room exceeds the designed alarming value, the alarming system in the control room should alarm automatically and start the chlorine recycling facilities. Then operator can search and find out the reasons as soon as possible and take any compensating measures to prevent accidents. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 153 

## 5 Public Consultation and Information Disclosure 

## 5.1 Purpose of Public Consultation 

Purpose of public Consultation is to obtain opinions of the people in areas directly affected by the project; to make it possible for the decision making department to find out hidden problems in time; to modify and improve the design timely; to solve from the root the problems reflected by the people; furthermore, to make the process design, environment protection measure, environment supervision and management better and reasonable; Therefore to pursue optimum unity of the environment profits, social profits and economic profits of the project. 

## 5.2 Approaches for Public Participation 

Environment assessment engineer or project PIU should first give the people living in the possibly affected area in details an introduction to the project to be constructed as well as the main results of the environment assessment, including project scale as well as short term or long term influences of the project to be constructed on the people and environment during its construction and operation etc so as to know about 

people’s attitude, requirements and suggestions to the project to be constructed. Methods of public consultation mainly refer to: Visit the area affected by the project construction; Communicate with the people and distribute public participating investigation form Individual interview, house-visiting interview and group interview (forum); Visiting and negotiating in sensitive areas Think about the impact of the road construction in Banqiao town, Yongchuan District, to the buddist activity place, Benzun Monastery, CPMO and environment assessment experts of Chongqing city visited the planning and construction team of the Benzun Monastery Buddhism place, Management Office of Culture and Historical Residue of Yongchuan District and Bureau of Nationality and Issues and National and Religious Affairs Bureau as well as went to the Benzun Monastery to ask for advices of part of the devout baptists of the budism folk organization “Fuyuan Club”, “Jixian club”. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 154 Comprehensive memo of public consultation and Infomation Disclosure for sub-components refer to table 5-1. Living pictures of real situation for Information Disclosure refer to attached file D.

## 5.5 Information Disclosure 5.5.1 Means of Information Disclosure 

Two times of EIA information disclosure about the sub-components of road, water supply, new countryside demonstration village construction and community health care system in Yubei District under CURIP has been publicized. The first time is not long after the environment problems selections combining the construction and real environment. The second is after the initial EA report draft and before the final report finishes. The ways applicable include publicizing at website and on the announcement board. The initial draft of the whole EA report has been publicized in the second time of information disclosure for public viewing. Live records of the Information Disclosure of each sub-components refer to attachment D. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 168 

## 5.5.2 Feedback and Responses 5.5.2.1 Feedback 

Most of the feedback after Information Disclosure comes from the construction team of Buddhism Relics of Benzun Monastery of Ban Qiao Town, Yongchuan District, 

the surrounding folk Buddhism organization “Fu Yuan Club”, “Ji Xian Club” and Bureau of National Religion Affair of Yongchuan District, main ideas are as below: The Benzun Monastery section of No.2 road shall not affect the buildings and students and teachers of Benzun School during its construction and operation. It is suggested that the road should go straight along the eastern wall of Benzun Primary School. 

5.5.2.2 Replies to the feedback 

For the feedback of “The No.2 road should go straight along the eastern wall of Benzun Primary School.”, the EA expert and the PIUs have surveyed the site and given the following replies: To avoid any potential negative impact on the buildings and pedestrians as well as students and teachers during construction and operation, the original routine in FSR has been dropped, and it is recommended that the new routine should go straight along the eastern wall of Benzun Primary School. Despite of increasing land occupation and investment, the new selection of routine is better because it has an outstanding social benefits. ## 6.2 Environment protection management plan 

PIUs of all sub-components under CURIP should make environment management plan covering construction and operation periods according to the construction features and environment features of the sub-component itself, main including: 

  Set up a complete environment management organization with clear goals and measurable indicators for each department so as to promote an all-round participation in the environment protection; 

  Make clear the duties and responsibilities of special environment protection staffs, put into place rules and regulations for environmental protection and supervise the implementation of them, such as job duties, operation manual, safety rules and management rules for environment facilities. Give training to the staffs regularly or occasionally about the environment knowledge and improve their sense for environment protection so as to ensure the success of the environment management and environment protection. 

  Implement the “3 meanwhile” rules in environment protection, guarantee the investment for environment protection, make sure the implementation in line with the design and carry forward the construction of environment protection facilities so as to ensure that environmental protection project can yield desired results. 

  Enhance supervising and management of environment protection facilities and promote overhaul and maintenance of the facilities to make sure normal operation of the facilities. Continuously improve management level of environment protection facilities. 

  Set up emergency treatment pre -plans for pollution risk preventing and environment pollution accidents according to features of the construction and the environment. 

  Set u p archives for pollution resources Please see Table 6-3 for Plan of Integrated Environmental protection measures for the CURIP. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 174 Table 6-3 Plan of integrated environmental protection measures for the CURIP

Design of location and route Compare and select the overall or partial schemes by integrating environment, social economy and engineering etc; Select the optimal plan which also shall be approved by related departments of governments of cities, districts and counties as well as the PIUs of sub-components;  Design agencies and EIA agencies The CPMO, development and reform commissions of districts and counties 2. Ecological protection measures The location and route of roads and pipe network shall be at the wasteland, shrub land, low hills and dry land as much as possible, and take up less fertile land and high-quality forest land as little as possible; The temporary land use shall give priority to the land used for subgrade and other permanent auxiliary facilities. Make use of the forest land as little as possible especially the virgin forest land; Shrinkable side slopes are used for the subgrade of road section with fertile land. The retaining wall is used to protect the subgrade. Reduce the land for subgrade. The borderline of forest area is equipped with a thick greening zone for sound insulation, so as to reduce the effect on the small animals in the forest area; Greening or vertical greening in accordance with the natural landscape shall be provided on both sides of bridge. The greening project for Jinsha Middle Road in Tongliang County and Xiushan County  Mie’ao to Liangting highroad shall comply with the regulations in “Regulations for construction index of urban greening planning ” Urban construction No [1993]784, namely that the area of green space shall not be less than 20% of urban trunk road. For the remarkable natural side slope, try to maintain the original landform. For vegetation, try to recover the natural wild vegetation. For man-made side slope, don’t use the cement-laid stone masonry or concrete side slope. Both plants and concrete are used to protect the slope. It is necessary to use the local plant with a powerful root system, strong water fixing feature and without special geological requirements to protect the slope. If it is necessary to use the cement-laid stone masonry or concrete to build the side slope, both the top and bottom of the side slope shall be planted with local vines so that in Spring, Summer and Winter a “green barrier” can be formed to reduce the impact on the visual perception of passers by. If the high side slope is located in the mountain, it is necessary to make the side slope gentle to form a natural side slope, and recover the natural wild vegetation. If the side slope is located in farmland and village, both the plant and the concrete shall be used to protect the slope. If only the retaining wall can be used but the side slope is impossible due to the characteristics of project, local vines shall be planted at the bottom of the retaining wall. Set up obstruction guards on the bridge to prevent the vehicles from falling into the river. Soil Conservation Optimize the balance between excavation and filling of earth and rock, as well as the location of borrow site, dumping place, and the temporary stacking place for surface earth. For sensitive locations such as school, hospital, residential area etc, sprinkle times should be increased in dry and windy weather. For stock ground that easily get sprinkled with fine powder substance, the location of the ground should be chosen downwind to sensitive locations, wind prevention and covering measures should also be taken to reduce dust; For the transportation of bulk concrete, sand and lime, covering measures must be taken to reduce dust; Strengthen the maintenance of construction transportation machines and reduce exhaust gas discharge. construction supervision companies environmental protection bureaus, development and reform commissions of districts and counties 5. ## 6.7 EMP Implementation and Reporting 

Environmental Management Plan in each stage shall be put into practice by the PIUs of the sub-components described in Table 6-4, 6-5 and 6-6. The implementation schedule and results shall be reported to CPMO, EPB of participating district/county 

and Bank’s project manager of CURIP. In addition, as for the sub-components whose environmental impact assessment documents need approval of the Municipal Bureaus shall be reported to Chongqing Municipal Environmental Protection Bureau at the same time. Special attention shall be paid, those measures, budgetary estimate, progress and desired effects found in the course of implementation that may be used to prevent, reduce, ease or compensate the negative effects and improve environmental performances and not described in Environmental Management Plan. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 267 

## 6.8 Environmental Supervision Plan 

Environmental surveillance is mainly carried out by the CPMO and Bureau of Environmental Protection of District and County where the projects are located, in accordance with the specific performance results, public response and the requirements of the project manager of CURIP World Bank Load Project pilot project; in addition, they shall review the supplementary measures of the EMP reported by the executive unit of the EMP and offer prompt feedback. ## 6.9 Environmental Monitoring Plan 6.9.1 Objectives 

During the construction and implementation stages of sub-components under CURIP project, environmental monitoring is a main part of the EMP. The objectives of carrying out monitoring program are: (1) Confirm the conclusion proposed in Environmental Impact Report concerning the environmental effects of the proposed sub-components; (2) Determine the practical effects, confirm and evaluate the degrees of the anticipate negative effects; (3) Verify the validity and applicability of the environmental protection measures; (4) Provide basis for the environmental protection measures adopted in order to solve the problems of the negative effects beyond the conclusion of the Environmental Impact Assessment. 

## 6.9.2 Responsibility Organization 

The monitoring program of the sub-components under CURIP project is proposed to be carried out by the environmental monitoring stations where the sub-components are located in the commission of the PIUs. The monitoring organizations of the related sub-components are under the jurisdiction of the Chongqing Municipal Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 269 Environmental Protection Bureau, and they are responsible for the environmental monitoring in World Bank Loan Project - CSCP(Chongqing Small Cities Infrastructure Improvement Project). The monitoring organizations above have more experience on the requirements of the environmental monitoring required by Chongqing Municipal Environmental Protection Bureau, State Environmental Protection Administration of China and World Bank, and they have advanced equipments and all kinds of personals, so the environmental monitoring of the proposed items shall be carried out by the environmental monitoring stations where the sub-components are located in the commission of the sub-components PIUs, and be under the direction of the Chongqing Municipal Environmental Protection Bureau. 

## 6.9.3 Monitoring Plan 

a) Monitoring in the commission of the sub-component PIUs The responsibility units of the monitoring plan refer to the contractors of the sub-components in the construction period and the PIUs of the sub-components in the operation stage, and the monitoring shall be carried out in the commission of the responsibility units. The related environmental monitoring station of district and county receiving the commission of the contractors or the contractors of sub-components will carry out the sampling, conservation and analysis in accordance with the directives issued by State Environmental Protection Administration of China as well as the methods described in the standards. The monitoring tasks proposed for sub-components include monitoring on environmental noise, water quality and environmental air, the specific plans including time, frequency, place and parameter refer to Table 6-9 to Table 6-11. Investigation of recovery and protection of environment refers to Table 6-12. The environmental monitoring plan of the sub-components of establishment of community health services system in Yubei District refers to Table 6-13. Table 6-9 Environmental Air Monitoring Plan

In addition, the Contractor and construction supervision companies should also conduct monitoring routinely for self-inspection and improvement timely. Details are as follows: (1) Conduct noise monitoring around construction machinery and sensitive spot by portable noise meter; Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 275 (2) Inspect flying dust emission caused by construction temporary road, powdery material storage yard, spoil borrow area, earth surface disturbed and, truck loaded with materials through visual observation; (3) Inspect water quality of river, stream, irrigation channel, reservoir, and pound surrounding bridge construction site, tunnel construction site, construction plant and camp through observational method. Take measures through self-inspection to control existing pollution source and prevent potential pollution source promptly. c) Environmental monitoring report and personnel training for contractor and construction supervision unit Contractor and construction supervision unit should submit environmental monitoring report to Project Management Office regularly. In addition, when contamination accident erupts unexpectedly, report details to Project Management Office immediately so that Project Management Office and the Contractor can take pollution control measures appropriately, such as to authorize specialized monitoring organization for monitoring etc. Every person engaged in monitoring work from the Contractor and the Engineer must be trained before work. All the requirements for monitoring and report should be covered in the bidding and tendering documents of the Contractor and the Engineer , and written into contract agreement finally. 

## 6.9.4 Monitoring report 

Contractor and sub-component PIUs authorize the environmental monitoring station of district and county to carry out monitoring work according to national regulation and to submit compiled formal monitoring reports (with one report in construction and one in operation) in written form to Chongqing Project Management Office, Development and Reform Commission of District and County and Bureau of Environmental Protection of District and County. The reports serve as a basis for Chongqing Project Management Office, Bureau of Environmental Protection of Chongqing, Development and Reform Commission of Bureau of Environmental Protection of District and County and World Bank to assess the environmental protection performance at every stage and step up environmental protection measures if environment impact is worse than expected. Monitoring report system of sub-components of CURIP project refers to Fig. 6-2. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 276 Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 277 

## 6.9.5 Monitoring Cost Estimate 

During construction, monitoring cost of the contractor will be listed in environmental management plan; the cost incurred by routine monitoring of contractor and supervision personnel, including the procurement of portable sound grader, which has been covered in the tender price, should not show in the environmental assessment report repeatedly. 

## 6.10 Training Program 

Project contractor and construction supervision companies must strengthen self-inspection awareness of environmental protection. Even if self-inspection is not an end in itself, it helps shape the awareness of environmental protection, and facilitates the supervision function of environmental management and supervision unit. Thus for better self-inspection effect, contractor, supervision personnel and their superiors must receive training. See Table 6-14 for personnel training plan in detail. ## 7 Executive Summary 

## 7.1 Background and Overview of the Project 

The construction of CURIP pilot project is a development need of Chongqing City and the State, under which the sub-components are encouraged and supported by the State policies, also the important infrastructure needed urgently to build for local 

village by people’s governments of relevant counties and districts. CURIP firstly breaks the city-oriented infrastructure and public service structure, focused on overcoming inequality of public service enabled to solve such problems as: different rights, qualities, quantities, prices, etc. so as to provide more consumable public products to countryside, the rural economy will develop and income improve accordingly. The obvious rural-urban divide in the dual conflicts between large city and countryside of Chongqing will be gradually narrowed during the reform and development. It is good for promoting balanced development of urbanization of Chongqing City as well as building harmonious society. Implementation of CURIP accumulated experiences and built model for deepening and popularizing rural-urban development and reform for Chongqing and the State. Therefore, it is very necessary to build pilot project of CURIP. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 279 

## 7.2 Summary of Environmental Impacts 

(1) The selection of sites and lines for sub-components of CURIP project has better rationality Although most of the sub-components of CURIP project is to be rebuilt or extended according to designed road line or based on existing road or water supply plant, comparison of many alternatives for selection of road line and technology plan for water supply plant is also conducted, through comprehensive comparison and analysis in respect of technical economy, impact of ecological environment, energy consumption reduction, etc. recommended plan is put forward. It is estimated that the sites and lines selection of sub-components of CURIP project has better rationality. (2) Environment situation of sub-components of CURIP project are protected well Except the sites selection and surrounding areas of migrant workers employability upgrade project and community health service system construction project as well as partial section and its surrounding environment are subject to the environment of urban ecological system, the rest sub-components are subject to basic agricultural ecological system environment; roads to be planned along and water supply factory site and its surrounding areas hardly exist native vegetation; the covering area of the project is mostly of agricultural reclamation, without focused forest; furthermore, the vegetation are mainly scattered trees or bamboo jungle; in according with site exploration of environment assessment units and investigation to district or county-level environment protection authorities, there do not exist natural protection areas, scenic spots or wild animals protect by the State within in the assessment range; there is few wild animals effected by human being production and life; after site exploration and analysis for scenic data, each sub-component assessment area is characterized by good ecological environment quality, strong production capability and restorability after disturbance The sites selection of sub-components and road line located in rural area, except for environmental quality for noise of exceptional sub-components of Tongliang municipal road project which operation is conducted on section of the existing road are not in accordance with standard, because the sensitive point is near the road, environmental quality for noise, air and ecological of sites selection of other sub-components and road line are good, environmental quality for noise up to Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 280 

“Environmental quality Standard for Noise” (GB3096-2008) Class II, air up to “Ambient Air Quality Standard” (GB3095-1996) Class II. Existing quality conditions of surface water from water source or non-water source area related to water supply project is good, water quality from water source area meets Class I or II “Water Quality Standard for Drinking Water Sources” (CJ3020-93); surface water from non water source area meets Class III or II “Environmental Quality Standards for Surface Water” (GB3838-2002). (3) Ecological environment protective measures are applicable and practicable Implementation of sub-components as road, water supply of CURIP changes the existing situation of agricultural ecological system at site and line selection area into urban ecological system. But as the sub-components covered less ratio of local environment, character of agricultural ecological system and its surroundings is not to change significantly, except for land form, topographic feature and surface of partial section for sub-components, biodiversity, timber growing stock, surface water and groundwater will maintain the status quo; Due to setting of flood-relief river courses of sub-components as road, water supply and vegetation recovery after construction, water and soil loss will be relieved to some degree during the rainy season at the area the items located, soil and water conservation level is to be improved at the relevant areas; Except for recovering the vegetation damaged by construction, water supply also reinforced ecological environmental protection at the water source. Elementary health service system also can gradually improve situation that disposing medical waste disorderly in rural area resulting soil and water pollution. Analyzed generally, measures taken by sub-components under CURIP to protect ecological environment of the surroundings are practicable, ecological environmental impact during construction is not obvious, partial ecological environment of these areas can be promoted to some degree. (4) Less environment impact during construction and significant social economic benefits during operation Environmental impact during construction is not last long, except for removal of few migrants and partially change of surface features, most of the impacts are characterized by reversibility, and due to small scale of project and not suitable for large mechanical operation restricted by complicated condition in mountain-hill areas, reduced noise from machines and waste gas and dust impact on environment in Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 281 respect of degree and range. Through comprehensive preventing and management measures, destroy to ecology of the construction area reduced and recovered gradually after completion. Favorable environmental impact is significant, although emphasis of infrastructure platform construction of road and water supply, employability promotion of migrant workers and establishment of elementary health service system is different, all of which are necessary parts of rural-urban development and reform, even basis of basic life security and development urgently needed to solve by rural area. Construction of road can narrow the gap between the developed and undeveloped area, close the relationship between urban and rural area, which is good for mutual advantage complement and overall development as well as promotion of urban-rural integration, social benefits and economic benefits are obvious. Water supply project can not only solve the safe drinking water of the residents and basic life security in area where the project located, but is also good to improve and promote the living conditions in rural area and new countryside, at the same time, open the bottle-neck restraining the rural economic development, which has significant economic and social benefits. Establishment of migrant workers training base and rural health service system is an important measure for rural-urban development and reform, focusing practical training and health care on village that has more population and large demand is not only good for rural education, health basic infrastructure system building and reform, but also good for rural practical training, rational distribution and efficient utilization 

of health service resources; the gradually developed migrant workers’ team with high technique has become a necessary strength in promoting urban-rural integration; Establishment of rural health service system relieved the difficulties of seeing a doctor for rural area and village in aspect of hardware facilities, which can improve rational flow reform of health care providers in rural area and professional ability of elementary health care workers, at the same time, it is good for popularizing and promoting rural health care level, comprehensively improving health of the public. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 282 After implementation of the project, the population that directly benefits from the project will reach 61,660,000. In addition, from only 4 sub-components including employability promotion of migrant workers, accumulated population in 15 years that directly benefits will be 506,800, outpatient emergency of sub-component under Community Health Services System Yubei District in the near future will reach 422,500/a, which proves significant benefit of pilot project of CURIP. Besides, improvement of rural infrastructure as road and water supply will promote utilization of rural land for increment, water source protection, water resources and distribution will become more scientific and rational, water and soil conservation will also be improved. (5) Public actively support establishment of planed project It is proved by public consultation that people in relevant counties and areas actively support the planed project, understand and cooperate with removing and resettlement during construction; Environmental impact assessment organization and the PIU explained and answered the problems and ideas put forward by the public and was understood and supported by them. (6) Conclusion summarizing With rational sites and line selection, mature and reliable technology, sub-components under CURIP project have less pollutant, technology of which conforms to cleaner production requirements. Environmental investment for sub-components under CURIP project will be totally 33,732,000 Yuan (22,474,000 Yuan is counted as project investment ), which accounted for 2.6% of the total investment for pilot project of CURIP; There are practicable pollution control measures during the construction and operation of sub-components, measures for ecological environment protection are practicable and pollutants can be discharged up to standard; It has less influence to atmosphere, surface water, noise and ecological environment of relevant areas, which will not result in significant change to local environmental function of the project; The construction accords with National Industrial Policy and local 

socioeconomic development planning; There aren’t important environmental restrictive factors; After implementation of the project, infrastructure, employment and medical level will be improved, people's living standard will be also improved, social and economic benefits as well as model function for rural-urban development Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 283 and reform of the state will be promoted obviously. Completely comply with environmental assessment document and environment protection control measures as well as project control measures put forward by feasibility study report, strengthen environ mental management, strictly implement the “three-simultaneities” and pollutants discharging up to standard, the pilot project of CURIP is feasible in view of environmental protection. 

## 7.3 Findings and Recommendations 

  Implement mitigation measures in environmental management plan during 

construction so as to prevent excess development In August 2009, debris flow, landslide and flood disaster occurred resulting from rainstorm, except for reasons for destroying of surface geologic structure resulting from Wenchuan earthquake and rainstorm, excess development of some areas resulted in serious water and soil loss and vegetation destroy, soil and water conversation capability of earth's surface lost, which is also one of the reasons, whereas, implement mitigation measures in environmental management plan during construction of pilot project of CURIP so as to prevent secondary disaster resulting from excess development; 

  During water demand forecast for water supply project, it is suggested to consider 

as per 70L~80L/d advised by memorandum taken for many times by World Bank Mission for water consumption per person; 

  It is suggested that relevant district and county sho uld list in plan the treatment proposal for increased sewage in environmental assessment of sub-components: I The scale of sewage treatment plant planed by Huangshui Town does not match with water supply scale of Huangshui Town planed by CURIP (after practice of Phase I and II of the planed sewage treatment plant of Huangshui Town, sewage treatment scale is only 3300m 3/d, which can not treat the sewage increased by the supply project); The planed completion and production date of sewage treatment plant can not join with operation date of water supply project (former is 2012, later is November 2010); II .Environmental assessment suggestions on Xiushan water supply project: Bring the plan of building sewage treatment plant into the adjusting plan, carry out as soon as possible the scale, localization of plant, main technology, the PIU, capital, schedule, etc. it is suggested that Xiushan County hand in written documents about related Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 284 situation to relative plan and administrative department for discussion recently; III Environmental assessment suggestions on Zhuyi Town Water Supply Project in Fengjie County: plan and construct simultaneously a sewage treatment plant in Zhuyipian District with water supply project, sewage treatment capacity: 16,000~20,000 t/d, sewage after treatment would in accordance with Integrated Sewage Discharge Standard (GB8978-96) Class I discharge level; it is suggested that Fengjie County should hand in written documents about related situation to relative plan and administrative department for discussion recently; 

  Qijiang and Chengkou County are located in mountainous area, in respect to dispersing the increased sewage generated by construction of water supply project in new countryside, the assessment suggests to build biogas system to purify sewage in mode of individual farmer where the sewage is convenient to collect or group farmer; 

  For road project where operation will be conducted on existing road, temporary 

road should be constructed for passerby during construction of road bad and surface or alternate half width construction, with safety shield between the two halves; while waterlogging on the construction section or splashing water resulting from vehicles affected traffic, get rid of waterlogging, at the same time, vehicles should pass slowly; 

  300,000 Yuan used for landscape design of the environmental protection 

investment in the project of Qijiang new countryside construction is cancelled; Combine the edge road construct ruction of Qinglong Village road construction project in Xianyi Town, Chengkou County with flood control of Hanchang River; 

 It ’s recommended to build the wastewater treatment station of community health care guidance center in the south within red line area(see annex B); the elevation should be lower or close to height of bottom of Dashiba Reservoir which will be easy for treated wastewater going to urban sewage system and preventing the IV class basin from polluting. It’s recommended to transfer medical and domestic wastewater into purifying biogas pool for treatment and up-to-standard discharge in close proximity if the sewage is in small volume and easy to be treated. Those measures are feasible in technology and economy and applicable for small-scale rural healthcare stations, clinics and 10 community healthcare stations under Yubei subcomponent. 

  The construction unit should arrange electric pole and wire removing and Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 285 upgrading plan, all aspects including material, transportation, loading and unloading, security, even weather should be considered; Construction period should be noticed in advance, inform every family and shorten the period as far as possible; 

  Before new water supply project is used, although the existing facilities stop to 

supply water, which will bring many inconveniences to life and work of residents in its service; It is suggested that the design department arrange the replacement of existing water supply facilities by planed water supply project join perfectly, shorten replacement period as far as possible, control the replacement affects in certain range;

Public Consultation on EIA of Reconstruction of Huimin – Yipin Road in Banan District 

Public meetings, questionnaires and interviews were adopted for public consultation on EIA of reconstruction of Huimin-Yipin Road in Banan. The residents, the business owner and entities impacted by it are all the targets of such public opinion survey. Stage 1: March 5, 2009. The public were informed of the construction plan, the EIA report and environmental protection measures to be taken via public announcement, investigation and interview etc. in the regions impacted by this project. Their opinions and advice were collected. Stage 2: March 24-25, 2009. Informal discussion was convened to gather all representatives. The environmental impact assessor mainly introduced during the meeting the possible environmental impact of this planned project and the preventive 

measures to be taken. It also inquired about the representatives’ opinions on this project and on the measures to be taken.

5. Public Consultation on Huangshui Town Water Supply Project in Shizhu County 

Disclosure of EIA Information on Huangshui Town Water Supply Project in Shizhu County 

Site investigation in regions affected by the project and random survey via questionnaires on public participation were adopted for public consultation on EIA of Huangshui Town Water Supply Project in Shizhu County. There were three kinds of interviews: individual interview, household interview and group interview (informal discussion meeting). Investigator first introduced to the respondents the general situation of the project to be built, including the project size and the long-term and short-term impact of this project to the public and the environment during its construction and operation, to understand the public opinions, requirement and advice on the planned project. Then, the respondents volunteered to fill in the questionnaires on public participation or dictate their opinions which were recorded by investigators. Last, investigators sorted, summarized and analyzed the questionnaires collected. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 305 The assessors briefly disclosed to the public, on July 3, 2009 after they finished the first draft of EIA and in the bulletin boards of Shizhu Huangshui Water Supply 

Company and Huangshui Town People’s Government, the general situation on water supply project, possible adverse environmental impact, relevant environmental protection measures and expected effect. They also introduced the location of the full EIA report (Meeting Room of Huangshui Water Supply Company) and the contact information for the purpose of public consultation, reference and feedback.

They disclosed such information via posting announcements in bulletin boards and publicizing the 

“EIA information” at the Government’s website in order to exchange ideas and interact with the public and to inform the public of the EIA information more conveniently and accurately.

## Annex A Standards for EIA A1 Standards for Environmental Quality 

a) Ambient Air Class 2 standards of the Ambient Air Quality Standard (GB3095-1996) shall be the criteria for ambient air quality assessment. The Sanitary Standard for the Design of Industrial Enterprise (TJ 36-79) shall prevail in case of leak of liquid chlorine in the water supply sub-component; please see Table A-1 for standard values. The Discharge Standard of Water Pollutants for Medical Institution (GB 18466-2005) shall be also implemented if there are sewage treatment stations in medical institutions in sub-component IV (please see Table A-2). The maximum chlorine concentration allowed in the ambient air of Chlorination Room for the water supply sub-component shall be subject to GB 18466-2005.

Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 314 b) Acoustic Environment According to the area divisions in the Environmental Quality Standard for Noise 

(GB3096-2008) and the Circular of Chongqing Environmental Protection Bureau on Amending the Target Areas of Standard of Environmental Noise of Urban Area (YHF [2007] No. 78), Class 4a standards shall apply to the 30m-wide regions at both sides of the road (marked by red lines) since the buildings along the site of CURIP-related sub-components are almost lower than three floors and Class 2 standards apply to adjacent regions. Thus, the equivalent sound level shall be 70dBA in the daytime and 55dBA at night. Class 2 standards of the Standard of Environmental Noise of Urban Area (GB3096-2008) shall apply to other regions adjacent to the road, i.e. the equivalent sound level shall be 60dBA in the daytime and 50dBA at night. The Noise limits for Construction Site (GB12523-90) shall be adopted for assessment during the construction period. The noise and vibration shall be subject to Table A-3 and Table A-4. ## ANNEX C Road Infrastructure Project in Banqiao Town of Yongchuan City, Chongqing Municipality Cultural Heritage Benzun Monastery concerning No. 2 Road 

Investigation Report 

(Site investigation on July 9, 2009) Benzun Monastery, 4km away from Banqiao Town, is located in the center of 

Lianhuaba, a flat land like a “lotus flower” (“lianhua” in Chinese pinyin). And Liuxi River winds across the flat land. This monastery was built in the mid-Tang Dynasty and has lasted more than 1,000 years. It is said to be the tonsure place of an eminent monk named Liu Benzun and that is why this monastery is named Benzun Monastery . Benzun Monastery has a long-standing reputation in Yongchuan and surrounding districts and counties. Chongqing Huayan Monastery and this monastery are the sister monasteries since their elders are of the same seniority mark and from the same Buddhist division. The three large halls of Benzun Monastery, Tianwang Hall, Guanyin Hall and Daxiong Hall, the three small halls, Yong’an Hall, Chenghuang Hall and Manjusri Samantabhadra Hall, Shiwang Hall and Bell & Drum Pavilion no longer exist due to historical reasons, and several Buddha statues (Fig. 2) are kept only in some rooms of Benzun Monastery (Fig. 1), and some believers have built, at their own cost, another two small halls (Fig. 3) at this historic site. The morning chimes and evening prayer are occasionally heard in Benzun Monastery and the pilgrims are much less now than in the period when it was flourishing. However, thanks to its long-lasting culture of Buddhist, some non-governmental organizations, including “Fuyuan Association” and “Jixian Association”, will organize the believers together on the birthday of Guanyin (February 19 in Chinese calendar) and on the Gala of Buddha (April 8 in Chinese calendar). They will carry the Buddha’s seat, wave colored flags and sing “Namo Amitabha”. The believers may pray for happiness, longevity, wealth or descendants, or confess a sin. In addition, various retailers and the flourishing amusement and service industries here make the festival jollier. Benzun Monastery, a historically valuable site, is classified as Yongchuan’s district-level cultural heritage. Chongqing Yongchuan People’s Government and Banqiao Town have planned to rebuild Benzun Monastery when appropriate to pass down the Buddhist culture and build a harmonious society. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 322 The clay-bound road in Fig. 3 and Fig. 4 is the existing Benzun Monastery section of No. 2 road. It is planned, before the site investigation by the CPMO and EIA experts, to expand this road and thus form the Benzun Monastery section of No. 2 road in sub-component of road infrastructure project in Banqiao Town of Yongchuan City, which will occupy the land owned by this monastery. Moreover, the existing Benzun Monastery section road leads to the newly built monastery gate and is along the central axis of some halls of Benzun Monastery. It follows that the expansion of this section will damage the traditional integrality of this monastery and cause serious religious disputes. Benzun Villagers, the Buddhist believers and Mr. Li, head of Yongchuan County Administration of Cultural Heritage, all supported the expansion and reconstruction of No. 2 Road during the investigation. They said that there were always traffic jams and large crowd once there were important Buddhist activities or on holidays and the expansion of road will make their life and work more convenient. Believers and tourists from the regions hundreds miles away can pass down such Buddhist culture as peace, luck, kin dness, “Tolerance brings respect”, “Cause breeds effect” etc. via exchange of religious culture to obtain soul inspiration. A head of Yongchuan County Administration of Ethnic and Religious Affairs emphasized that the planned restoration and reconstruction of Benzun Monastery must be taken into consideration during construction of road. The head also said they should abide by all the religious traditions in respect of Chinese Buddhist monasteries. The expansion of existing road will cause the road to cross the monastery and to occupy the new gate and central axis of several halls, which is a great taboo for Buddhist monasteries. Mdm. Fan, a member of the team of establishing Buddhist site in Benzun Monastery, also requested the experts and the PIU to consider the possible response of believers if the road occupies the land for the monastery. Through investigation, the CPMO and EIA experts strongly recommended the PIU to choose another route for Benzun Monastery section of No. 2 road, i.e. to detour Benzun Monastery to the east of this monastery. This is consistent with the World Bank’s comprehensive policies on cultural heritage. The World Bank will help protect cultural heritage and avoid damages in the projects funded by it; it will provide Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 323 protection and improvement for the cultural heritage involved instead of just leaving it as it is now. It is advised to choose another route for the project on certain conditions to protect, study on and restore the sites and buildings. The sub-component PIU also recognized, via investigation, the problems of the former route selected. It agreed to adopt the alternative to detour Benzun Monastery and to modify relevant documents as soon as possible. Benzun Monastery is an ancient cultural heritage with great historic values. The tablet (Fig. 5) excavated by believers from ruins indicates that Benzun Monastery is of significant importance for the study on Buddhist culture and on local culture and history. The contractor of this section shall prepare and submit detailed construction plan for protecting cultural heritage as one of the construction organization proposals, and abide by the same during management and supervision of construction. 

Fig. 1 District-Level Cultural Heritage-Benzun Monastery (The white building behind is the Public Service Center of Benzun Village.) Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 324 

Fig. 2 Statues of Manjusri Samantabhadra 

Fig. 3 Two Small Halls Built by Some Believers at their Own Cost Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 325

Public Consultation on EIA of Reconstruction of Huimin – Yipin Road in Banan District 

Public meetings, questionnaires and interviews were adopted for public consultation on EIA of reconstruction of Huimin-Yipin Road in Banan. The residents, the business owner and entities impacted by it are all the targets of such public opinion survey. Stage 1: March 5, 2009. The public were informed of the construction plan, the EIA report and environmental protection measures to be taken via public announcement, investigation and interview etc. in the regions impacted by this project. Their opinions and advice were collected. Stage 2: March 24-25, 2009. Informal discussion was convened to gather all representatives. The environmental impact assessor mainly introduced during the meeting the possible environmental impact of this planned project and the preventive measures to be taken. It also inqui red about the representatives’ opinions on this project and on the measures to be taken.

5. Public Consultation on Huangshui Town Water Supply Project in Shizhu County 

Disclosure of EIA Information on Huangshui Town Water Supply Project in Shizhu County 

Site investigation in regions affected by the project and random survey via questionnaires on public participation were adopted for public consultation on EIA of Huangshui Town Water Supply Project in Shizhu County. There were three kinds of interviews: individual interview, household interview and group interview (informal discussion meeting). Investigator first introduced to the respondents the general situation of the project to be built, including the project size and the long-term and short-term impact of this project to the public and the environment during its construction and operation, to understand the public opinions, requirement and advice on the planned project. Then, the respondents volunteered to fill in the questionnaires on public participation or dictate their opinions which were recorded by investigators. Last, investigators sorted, summarized and analyzed the questionnaires collected. Summary of Environmental Impact Assessment Report for CURIP 334 The assessors briefly disclosed to the public, on July 3, 2009 after they finished the first draft of EIA and in the bulletin boards of Shizhu Huangshui Water Supply 

Company and Huangshui Town People’s Government, the general situation on water supply project, possible adverse environmental impact, relevant environmental protection measures and expected effect. They also introduced the location of the full EIA report (Meeting Room of Huangshui Water Supply Company) and the contact information for the purpose of public consultation, reference and feedback.
